'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsTelUpTo = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsTelDesc'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsTelDesc = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsTelII'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsTelII = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
					IF(@prsTelII IS NOT NULL)
					BEGIN
						DECLARE @HavePermissionDuplicatePrsTelII INT
						IF EXISTS (
						SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
						WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmCompanyI' )
						AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actCanSaveTelDupAtTimeLimit'
											AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmCompanyI' ))
						AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
						SET @HavePermissionDuplicatePrsTelII = 1

						ELSE
						SET @HavePermissionDuplicatePrsTelII = 0

						IF(@HavePermissionDuplicatePrsTelII = 0 AND @CantSaveDupTel = 1)
						BEGIN

							/*AI14000304 - preTel + Tel for checking duplicate instead of Tel*/
							DECLARE @chckPrsTelII NVARCHAR(100)
							SET @chckPrsTel = ISNULL(@prsPreTel, '') + @prsTelII

							DECLARE @ResultCheckDuplicateForPrsTelII INT
							EXEC MobspCheckDuplicateTel @chckPrsTelII,2, 0,@LanguageID,@DefaultLanguageID /*AI14010329*/
							SET @ResultCheckDuplicateForPrsTelII = @@ROWCOUNT
							IF(@ResultCheckDuplicateForPrsTelII > 0)
								SET @IsDuplicate = 1
						END
					END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsFax'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsFax = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
					IF(@prsFax IS NOT NULL)
					BEGIN
						DECLARE @HavePermissionDuplicatePrsFax INT
						IF EXISTS (
						SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
						WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmCompanyI' )
						AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actCanSaveFaxDupAtTimeLimit'
											AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmCompanyI' ))
						AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
						SET @HavePermissionDuplicatePrsFax = 1

						ELSE
						SET @HavePermissionDuplicatePrsFax = 0

						IF(@HavePermissionDuplicatePrsFax = 0 AND @CantSaveDupCmpFax = 1)
						BEGIN

							/*AI14000304 - preTel + Tel for checking duplicate instead of Tel*/
							DECLARE @chckPrsFax NVARCHAR(100)
							SET @chckPrsTel = ISNULL(@prsPreTel, '') + @prsFax

							DECLARE @ResultCheckDuplicateForPrsFax INT
							EXEC MobspCheckDuplicateTel @chckPrsFax,2, 0,@LanguageID,@DefaultLanguageID /*AI14010329*/
							SET @ResultCheckDuplicateForPrsFax = @@ROWCOUNT
							IF(@ResultCheckDuplicateForPrsFax > 0)
								SET @IsDuplicate = 1
						END
					END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsFaxDesc'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsFaxDesc = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsMobile'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsMobile = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END						
					IF(@prsMobile IS NOT NULL)
					BEGIN
						DECLARE @HavePermissionDuplicatePrsMobile INT
						IF EXISTS (
						SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
						WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmCompanyI' )
						AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actCanSaveMobDupAtTimeLimit'
											AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmCompanyI' ))
						AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
						SET @HavePermissionDuplicatePrsMobile = 1

						ELSE
						SET @HavePermissionDuplicatePrsMobile = 0

						IF(@HavePermissionDuplicatePrsMobile = 0 AND @CantSaveDupPrsMob = 1)
						BEGIN
							DECLARE @ResultDupcmpNumber NVARCHAR(50) = ''
							EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicatePrsMobile]
								@prsMobile = @prsMobile, 
								@CurrId = 0,
								@ResultDupcmpNumber = @ResultDupcmpNumber OUTPUT 
							IF(@ResultDupcmpNumber <> '')
								SET @IsDuplicate = 1
						END
					END														
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsWebSite'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsWebSite = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsEmail'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsEmail = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END						
					/*check CantSaveDupPrsEmail*/
					IF(@CantSaveDupPrsEmail = 1 AND @prsEmail IS NOT NULL)
					BEGIN
						DECLARE @ResultDupcmpNumberForPrsEmail NVARCHAR(50) = ''
						EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicatePrsEmail]
						@prsEmail = @prsEmail, 
						@CurrId = 0,
						@ResultDupcmpNumber = @ResultDupcmpNumberForPrsEmail OUTPUT 
	
							
						IF(@ResultDupcmpNumberForPrsEmail <> '')
							SET @IsDuplicate = 1						 																						
					END									
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsEmailII'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsEmailII = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END						
					/*check CantSaveDupPrsEmail*/
					IF(@CantSaveDupPrsEmail = 1 AND @prsEmailII IS NOT NULL)
					BEGIN
						DECLARE @ResultDupcmpNumberForPrsEmailII NVARCHAR(50) = ''
						EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicatePrsEmail]
						@prsEmail = @prsEmailII, 
						@CurrId = 0,
						@ResultDupcmpNumber = @ResultDupcmpNumberForPrsEmailII OUTPUT 
	
							
						IF(@ResultDupcmpNumberForPrsEmailII <> '')
							SET @IsDuplicate = 1						 																						
					END					
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsCountryId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @prsCountryId = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsProvinceId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @prsProvinceId = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsCityId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @prsCityId = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsAddress'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsAddress = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsAddressDesc'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsAddressDesc = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsZipCode'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsZipCode = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
					IF(@prsZipCode IS NOT NULL)
					BEGIN
						IF(@CantSaveDupCmpZipCode = 1)
						BEGIN
							DECLARE @ResultCheckDuplicateForPrsZipCode INT
							EXEC MobspCheckDuplicateZipCode @prsZipCode,2, 0,@LanguageID,@DefaultLanguageID /*AI14010329*/
							SET @ResultCheckDuplicateForPrsZipCode = @@ROWCOUNT
							IF(@ResultCheckDuplicateForPrsZipCode > 0)
								SET @IsDuplicate = 1
						END
					END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsPostBox'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsPostBox = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsNationalCode'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsNationalCode = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
					IF(@prsNationalCode IS NOT NULL)
					BEGIN
						IF(@CantSaveDupPrsNationalCode = 1)
						BEGIN
						/**/
							DECLARE @ResultCheckDuplicateForPrsNationalCode INT
							EXEC MobspCheckDuplicatePrsNationalCode @prsNationalCode,0,@LanguageID,@DefaultLanguageID /*AI14010329*/
							SET @ResultCheckDuplicateForPrsNationalCode = @@ROWCOUNT
							IF(@ResultCheckDuplicateForPrsNationalCode > 0)
								SET @IsDuplicate = 1
						/**/
						END
					END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsIdentityNumber'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsIdentityNumber = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsFatherName'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsFatherName = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsBirthDate'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsBirthDate = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE REPLACE(@PrsFieldValue, '/', '') END	
					/*RZ961206- Add time to date*/						
					SET @prsBirthDateTime = CASE WHEN @prsBirthDate IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@prsBirthDate, @RegisterTime) END			 	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsBirthPlace'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsBirthPlace = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsIsMarried'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsIsMarried = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL 
										WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'false' THEN 0 
										ELSE 1 END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsMarriageDate'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsMarriageDate = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE REPLACE(@PrsFieldValue, '/', '') END
					/*RZ961206- Add time to date*/								
					SET @prsMarriageDateTime = CASE WHEN @prsMarriageDate IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@prsMarriageDate, @RegisterTime) END			 	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsEducationField'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsEducationField = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsSkill'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsSkill = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsStudyField'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsStudyField = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsInteresting'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsInteresting = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsSpecialPoint'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsSpecialPoint = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsDescription'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsDescription = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsImportDate'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsImportDate = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE REPLACE(@PrsFieldValue, '/', '') END
					/*RZ961206- Add time to date*/							
					SET @prsImportDateTime = CASE WHEN @prsImportDate IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@prsImportDate, @RegisterTime) END			 	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsFirstRegisterUserId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @prsFirstRegisterUserId = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsDepartmentId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @prsDepartmentId = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsStopSendSms'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsStopSendSms = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL 
										WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'false' THEN 0 
										ELSE 1 END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsOrderView'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @prsOrderView = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsWorkingOut'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsWorkingOut = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL 
										WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'false' THEN 0 
										ELSE 1 END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'CompanyPersonTemplateID'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @CompanyPersonTemplateID = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsFirstRegisterDatePersian'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsFirstRegisterDatePersian = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE REPLACE(@PrsFieldValue, '/', '') END	
					/*RZ961206- Add time to date*/						
					SET @prsFirstRegisterDate = CASE WHEN @prsFirstRegisterDatePersian IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@prsFirstRegisterDatePersian, @RegisterTime) END			 	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				/*IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsTelegramId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @prsTelegramId = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS INT) END
				END	*/
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'PersonPicture'
				BEGIN
					/*SET @PersonPicture = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS VARBINARY(MAX)) END	*/
					/*RZ961017- convert image*/
					SET @PersonPicture = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE 
						(SELECT CAST(N'' AS xml).value('xs:base64Binary(sql:variable("@PrsFieldValue"))', 'varbinary(max)'))
					END
				END							
				/*END PERSON*/		
				/*END GENERAL*/
				/*---------------------------- END Fill variabele --------------------------------*/
				/*---------------------------- Remove current parameter from listparam ------------*/
				SET @PAllFields = SUBSTRING(@PAllFields,
                                  LEN(@PrsField + '|#|') + 1,
                                  LEN(@PAllFields))                                                                                                      
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				SET @PrsField = @PAllFields
				SET @PAllFields = NULL
				/*SELECT @CmpField*/
			END
		END  
		/************* Insert to tmp table***************/
		INSERT INTO @PersonTmpTable 
				 (
					[CompanyPersonID],
					[prsCompanyId],
					[prsLName],
					[prsFName],
					[prsTitleId],
					[prsJobId],
					[prsTypeId],
					[prsGroupId],
					[prsSexId],
					[prsEducationId],
					[prsPreTel],
					[prsTel],
					[prsTelUpTo],
					[prsTelDesc],
					[prsTelII],
					[prsFax],
					[prsFaxDesc],
					[prsMobile],
					[prsWebSite],
					[prsEmail],
					[prsEmailII],
					[prsCountryId],
					[prsProvinceId],
					[prsCityId],
					[prsAddress],
					[prsAddressDesc],
					[prsZipCode],
					[prsPostBox],
					[prsNationalCode],
					[prsIdentityNumber],
					[prsFatherName],
					[prsBirthDate],
					[prsBirthDateTime],
					[prsBirthPlace],
					[prsIsMarried],
					[prsMarriageDate],
					[prsMarriageDateTime],
					[prsEducationField],
					[prsSkill],
					[prsStudyField],
					[prsInteresting],
					[prsSpecialPoint],
					[prsDescription],
					[prsImportDate],
					[prsImportDateTime],
					[prsDepartmentId],
					[prsStopSendSms],
					[prsOrderView],
					[prsWorkingOut],
					[CompanyPersonTemplateID],
					[prsFirstRegisterUserId],
					[prsFirstRegisterDatePersian],
					[prsTelegramId],
					[prsFirstRegisterDate],
					[PersonPicture]
				)
				VALUES
				(
					@CompanyPersonID,
					@prsCompanyId,
					@prsLName,
					@prsFName,
					@prsTitleId,
					@prsJobId,
					@prsTypeId,
					@prsGroupId,
					@prsSexId,
					@prsEducationId,
					@prsPreTel,
					@prsTel,
					@prsTelUpTo,
					@prsTelDesc,
					@prsTelII,
					@prsFax,
					@prsFaxDesc,
					@prsMobile,
					@prsWebSite,
					@prsEmail,
					@prsEmailII,
					@prsCountryId,
					@prsProvinceId,
					@prsCityId,
					@prsAddress,
					@prsAddressDesc,
					@prsZipCode,
					@prsPostBox,
					@prsNationalCode,
					@prsIdentityNumber,
					@prsFatherName,
					@prsBirthDate,
					@prsBirthDateTime,
					@prsBirthPlace,
					@prsIsMarried,
					@prsMarriageDate,
					@prsMarriageDateTime,
					@prsEducationField,
					@prsSkill,
					@prsStudyField,
					@prsInteresting,
					@prsSpecialPoint,
					@prsDescription,
					@prsImportDate,
					@prsImportDateTime,
					@prsDepartmentId,
					@prsStopSendSms,
					@prsOrderView,
					@prsWorkingOut,
					@CompanyPersonTemplateID,
					@prsFirstRegisterUserId,
					@prsFirstRegisterDatePersian,
					@prsTelegramId,
					@prsFirstRegisterDate,
					@PersonPicture
				)                                              
		/*END PERSON*/		
        /*END GENERAL*/
        /*---------------------------- END Fill fields ------------------------------------*/
        /*---------------------------- Remove current parameter from listparam ------------*/
        SET @PersonList = SUBSTRING(@PersonList,
                                  LEN(@PrsAllFields + '|^#^|') + 1,
                                  LEN(@PersonList))                                  
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SET @PrsAllFields = @PersonList
        SET @PersonList = NULL
        /*SELECT @CmpField*/
    END
END
END
ELSE
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=2' 		

/**/
IF (@@ERROR > 0)
BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Input Person' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
ELSE 
COMMIT TRAN 
/**/

/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** Insert Pesons ***********************************/
/********************************************************************************/
DECLARE @InsertPrsId INT, @InsertedPrsIdsInMobAndDb NVARCHAR(500) = ''
BEGIN TRAN
/**/
/*check company is inserted*/

	IF (@PersonList IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
	IF(@IsDuplicate = 1)/* AND @TypeTable <> 'Dup')*/ /* @IsDuplicate = 1*/
	BEGIN
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'Dup=1Msg=0'	                           
	END
	ELSE IF(@ErrorMessage IS NULL OR  @ErrorMessage = '')/* @IsDuplicate = 0 ====> insert to main table*/
	BEGIN
		 DECLARE CrsrPrs Cursor For
				SELECT 
					[CompanyPersonID], [prsCompanyId], [prsLName], [prsFName], [prsTitleId], [prsJobId], [prsTypeId], [prsGroupId], [prsSexId],
					[prsEducationId], [prsPreTel], [prsTel], [prsTelUpTo], [prsTelDesc], [prsTelII], [prsFax], [prsFaxDesc], [prsMobile], [prsWebSite],
					[prsEmail], [prsEmailII], [prsCountryId], [prsProvinceId], [prsCityId], [prsAddress], [prsAddressDesc], [prsZipCode], [prsPostBox],
					[prsNationalCode], [prsIdentityNumber], [prsFatherName], [prsBirthDate], [prsBirthDateTime], [prsBirthPlace], [prsIsMarried], [prsMarriageDate],
					[prsMarriageDateTime], [prsEducationField], [prsSkill], [prsStudyField], [prsInteresting], [prsSpecialPoint], [prsDescription], [prsImportDate],
					[prsImportDateTime], [prsFirstRegisterUserId], [prsDepartmentId], [prsStopSendSms], [prsOrderView], [prsWorkingOut], [CompanyPersonTemplateID],
					[prsFirstRegisterUserId], [prsFirstRegisterDatePersian], [prsTelegramId], [prsFirstRegisterDate], [PersonPicture] 
				FROM @PersonTmpTable		
			/**/
			OPEN CrsrPrs
			FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrPrs INTO @CompanyPersonID, @prsCompanyId, @prsLName, @prsFName, @prsTitleId, @prsJobId, @prsTypeId, @prsGroupId,
										 @prsSexId, @prsEducationId, @prsPreTel, @prsTel, @prsTelUpTo, @prsTelDesc, @prsTelII, @prsFax, @prsFaxDesc,
										 @prsMobile, @prsWebSite, @prsEmail, @prsEmailII, @prsCountryId, @prsProvinceId, @prsCityId, @prsAddress,
										 @prsAddressDesc, @prsZipCode, @prsPostBox, @prsNationalCode, @prsIdentityNumber, @prsFatherName, @prsBirthDate,
										 @prsBirthDateTime, @prsBirthPlace, @prsIsMarried, @prsMarriageDate, @prsMarriageDateTime, @prsEducationField,
										 @prsSkill, @prsStudyField, @prsInteresting, @prsSpecialPoint, @prsDescription, @prsImportDate, @prsImportDateTime,
										 @prsFirstRegisterUserId, @prsDepartmentId, @prsStopSendSms, @prsOrderView, @prsWorkingOut, @CompanyPersonTemplateID,
										 @prsFirstRegisterUserId, @prsFirstRegisterDatePersian, @prsTelegramId, @prsFirstRegisterDate, @PersonPicture
			WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
			BEGIN
			/**/
				IF(@TypeTable IS NULL OR @TypeTable = '')
				BEGIN
					DECLARE @CompanyID INT
					SET @CompanyID = (SELECT TOP 1 CompanyID FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @prsCompanyId)
					IF(@prsCompanyId = @CompanyID AND @CompanyID IS NOT NULL)
					BEGIN
						INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons]
						   ([prsCompanyId]
						   ,[prsLName]
						   ,[prsFName]
						   ,[prsTitleId]
						   ,[prsJobId]
						   ,[prsTypeId]
						   ,[prsGroupId]
						   ,[prsSexId]
						   ,[prsEducationId]
						   ,[prsPreTel]
						   ,[prsTel]
						   ,[prsTelUpTo]
						   ,[prsTelDesc]
						   ,[prsTelII]
						   ,[prsFax]
						   ,[prsFaxDesc]
						   ,[prsMobile]
						   ,[prsWebSite]
						   ,[prsEmail]
						   ,[prsEmailII]
						   ,[prsCountryId]
						   ,[prsProvinceId]
						   ,[prsCityId]
						   ,[prsAddress]
						   ,[prsAddressDesc]
						   ,[prsZipCode]
						   ,[prsPostBox]
						   ,[prsNationalCode]
						   ,[prsIdentityNumber]
						   ,[prsFatherName]
						   ,[prsBirthDate]
						   ,[prsBirthDateTime]
						   ,[prsBirthPlace]
						   ,[prsIsMarried]
						   ,[prsMarriageDate]
						   ,[prsMarriageDateTime]
						   ,[prsEducationField]
						   ,[prsSkill]
						   ,[prsStudyField]
						   ,[prsInteresting]
						   ,[prsSpecialPoint]
						   ,[prsDescription]
						   ,[UserId]
						   ,[Year1]
						   ,[Month1]
						   ,[RegisterDate]
						   ,[RegisterDatePersian]
						   ,[ImportDate]
						   ,[ImportDateTime]
						   ,[prsFirstRegisterUserId]
						   ,[prsDepartmentId]
						   ,[prsStopSendSms]
						   ,[prsOrderView]
						   ,[prsWorkingOut]
						   ,[CompanyPersonTemplateID]
						   ,[AppType]
						   ,[LanguageID]
						   ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
						   ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
						   ,[prsTelegramId]
						   ,[FirstRegisterDate])
					 VALUES
						   (@CompanyID
						   ,@prsLName
						   ,@prsFName
						   ,@prsTitleId
						   ,@prsJobId
						   ,@prsTypeId
						   ,@prsGroupId
						   ,@prsSexId
						   ,@prsEducationId
						   ,@prsPreTel
						   ,@prsTel
						   ,@prsTelUpTo
						   ,@prsTelDesc
						   ,@prsTelII
						   ,@prsFax
						   ,@prsFaxDesc
						   ,@prsMobile
						   ,@prsWebSite
						   ,@prsEmail
						   ,@prsEmailII
						   ,@prsCountryId
						   ,@prsProvinceId
						   ,@prsCityId
						   ,@prsAddress
						   ,@prsAddressDesc
						   ,@prsZipCode
						   ,@prsPostBox
						   ,@prsNationalCode
						   ,@prsIdentityNumber
						   ,@prsFatherName
						   ,@prsBirthDate
						   ,@prsBirthDateTime
						   ,@prsBirthPlace
						   ,@prsIsMarried
						   ,@prsMarriageDate
						   ,@prsMarriageDateTime
						   ,@prsEducationField
						   ,@prsSkill
						   ,@prsStudyField
						   ,@prsInteresting
						   ,@prsSpecialPoint
						   ,@prsDescription
						   ,@UserId
						   ,@Year1
						   ,@Month1
						   ,@RegisterDate
						   ,@RegisterDatePersian
						   ,@RegisterDatePersian /*AI14010621 - @prsImportDate*/
						   ,@RegisterDate /*AI14010621 - @prsImportDateTime*/
						   ,@prsFirstRegisterUserId
						   ,@prsDepartmentId
						   ,@prsStopSendSms
						   ,@prsOrderView
						   ,@prsWorkingOut
						   ,@CompanyPersonTemplateID
						   ,@AppType
						   ,@DefaultLanguageID
						   ,@prsFirstRegisterUserId
						   ,@RegisterDatePersian   /*AI14010621 - @prsFirstRegisterDatePersian*/
						   ,@prsTelegramId
						   ,@RegisterDate) /*AI14010621 - @prsFirstRegisterDate*/
						SET @InsertPrsId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()					
						/******* Insert to lang table ********/
						INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang]
						   ([CompanyPersonID]
						   ,[LanguageID]
						   ,[prsLName]
						   ,[prsFName]
						   ,[prsTelDesc]
						   ,[prsFaxDesc]
						   ,[prsAddress]
						   ,[prsAddressDesc]
						   ,[prsFatherName]
						   ,[prsBirthPlace]
						   ,[prsEducationField]
						   ,[prsSkill]
						   ,[prsStudyField]
						   ,[prsInteresting]
						   ,[prsSpecialPoint]
						   ,[prsDescription])     
						SELECT
							@InsertPrsId
						   ,LN.LanguageID
						   ,@prsLName
						   ,@prsFName
						   ,@prsTelDesc
						   ,@prsFaxDesc
						   ,@prsAddress
						   ,@prsAddressDesc
						   ,@prsFatherName
						   ,@prsBirthPlace
						   ,@prsEducationField
						   ,@prsSkill
						   ,@prsStudyField
						   ,@prsInteresting
						   ,@prsSpecialPoint
						   ,@prsDescription
						FROM dbo.Languages LN 
						WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
						/*RZ961028- check deactive lang*/
						AND LN.IsActive =1
						/******* Insert to picture table *****/
						IF(@PersonPicture IS NOT NULL)
						BEGIN
							INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmPersonPicture]
								   ([CompanyPersonId]
								   ,[PersonPicture]
								   ,[UserId]
								   ,[Year1]
								   ,[Month1]
								   ,[RegisterDate]
								   ,[RegisterDatePersian]
								   ,[AppType]
								   ,[LanguageID]
								   ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
								   ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
								   ,[FirstRegisterDate])
							 VALUES
								   (@InsertPrsId
								   ,@PersonPicture
								   ,@UserId
								   ,@Year1
								   ,@Month1
								   ,@RegisterDate
								   ,@RegisterDatePersian
								   ,@AppType
								   ,@DefaultLanguageID
								   ,@prsFirstRegisterUserId
								   ,@RegisterDatePersian /*AI14010621 - @prsFirstRegisterDatePersian*/
								   ,@RegisterDate) /*AI14010621 - @prsFirstRegisterDate*/
						END
						/** Auto send SMS after save mobile **/
						/*RZ971113- Auto send SMS after save mobile*/
						IF(@prsMobile IS NOT NULL AND @prsMobile <> '')
						BEGIN
							EXEC [dbo].[MobspAutoSendSMSAfterSaveMobile]
								@CompanyId =  @CompanyID,
								@CompanyPersonId = @InsertPrsId,
								@MobileNumber = @prsMobile,
								@UserId = @UserId,
								@AppType =@AppType,
								@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,
								@SendNeedCheck =  0
						END										
						/*RZ971113- END*/
						/***************/
						/*AI991008*/
						DECLARE @mainPersonId INT
						SET @mainPersonId = 
						(SELECT TOP 1 cmpMainPersonId
						FROM CrmCompany 
						WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyID)
						IF(@mainPersonId IS NULL)
						BEGIN
							UPDATE CrmCompany
							SET cmpMainPersonId = @InsertPrsId
							WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyID
						END
						/****************/
						/*************** Ids inserted ********/				
						/*SET @InsertedPersonIds = @InsertedPersonIds  + CAST(@InsertPrsId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '|'*/
						SET @InsertedPrsIdsInMobAndDb = @InsertedPrsIdsInMobAndDb + CAST(@CompanyPersonID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ',' + CAST(@InsertPrsId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '|'
						SET @InsertedPersonIds = @InsertedPrsIdsInMobAndDb
					END
					ELSE
						SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '<PRSID>'+ CAST(@CompanyPersonID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '<\PRSID>' + 'Msg=1'																		 
				END
				ELSE /*RZ961006- Remove insert in tmpTables*/
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'Dup=1Msg=0'
																		
			/**/					 					
			FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrPrs INTO @CompanyPersonID, @prsCompanyId, @prsLName, @prsFName, @prsTitleId, @prsJobId, @prsTypeId, @prsGroupId,
										 @prsSexId, @prsEducationId, @prsPreTel, @prsTel, @prsTelUpTo, @prsTelDesc, @prsTelII, @prsFax, @prsFaxDesc,
										 @prsMobile, @prsWebSite, @prsEmail, @prsEmailII, @prsCountryId, @prsProvinceId, @prsCityId, @prsAddress,
										 @prsAddressDesc, @prsZipCode, @prsPostBox, @prsNationalCode, @prsIdentityNumber, @prsFatherName, @prsBirthDate,
										 @prsBirthDateTime, @prsBirthPlace, @prsIsMarried, @prsMarriageDate, @prsMarriageDateTime, @prsEducationField,
										 @prsSkill, @prsStudyField, @prsInteresting, @prsSpecialPoint, @prsDescription, @prsImportDate, @prsImportDateTime,
										 @prsFirstRegisterUserId, @prsDepartmentId, @prsStopSendSms, @prsOrderView, @prsWorkingOut, @CompanyPersonTemplateID,
										 @prsFirstRegisterUserId, @prsFirstRegisterDatePersian, @prsTelegramId, @prsFirstRegisterDate, @PersonPicture		
			END
			CLOSE CrsrPrs
			DEALLOCATE CrsrPrs    					 	 	 
	END
	END
	
/**/
IF (@@ERROR > 0)
BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert Person' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
ELSE 
COMMIT TRAN 
/**/
/**/
/**/
IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
BEGIN 
	RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
 	SELECT 0
END 
ELSE
BEGIN
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
	SELECT 1
END	
/**/
END






GO

/*
	declare @p11 nvarchar(max)
	set @p11=N'1,8870|'
	declare @p12 nvarchar(max)
	set @p12=N''
	EXEC [dbo].[MobspInsertQuickProject] 
	@ProjectList=N'ProjectID=1|#|prjCompanyId=6929|#|prjTitle=www|#|prjDesc=|#|prjStartDate=1400/02/02|#|prjStartDay=5|#|prjStartTime=12:23:07|#|prjStarterUserId=3|#|prjFinishedStatusId=null|#|prjIsFinished=false|#|prjFinishedDate=|#|prjFinishedDay=null|#|prjFinishedTime=|#|prjFinishedUserId=null|#|prjGradeId=null|#|prjPriorityId=null|#|prjFirstRegisterUserId=3|#|prjGroupId=null|#|prjcnvsStatusGroupId=1|#|prjOrderView=null|#|prjPostFix=|#|prjFirstRegisterDatePersian=1400/02/02|#|prjRemindDate=|#|prjInsertForbidden=null|#|prjFOCmpCanSee=true|#|prjIsTicket=null|#|prjUserDepartmentId=null|#||^#^|',
	@UserId=3
	,@Year1=30
	,@Month1=2
	,@RegisterDate='2021-04-22 12:24:35'
	,@RegisterDatePersian=N'14000202'
	,@AppType='mob'

	,@DefaultLanguageID=1065
	,@TypeTable=NULL
	,@InsertedProjectIds=@p11 output,@ErrorMessage=@p12 output
	select @p11, @p12
*/
(
@ProjectList NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
@UserId INT,
@Year1 SMALLINT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType VARCHAR(20), 
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
@TypeTable NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
/**/
@InsertedProjectIds NVARCHAR(1000) OUTPUT,
@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** TRANSACTION *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/***************************** Define fields ************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
/************************************ Current Year ******************************/
DECLARE @CurrYear SMALLINT
SET @CurrYear = CAST(SUBSTRING(@RegisterDatePersian, 1, 4) AS SMALLINT)	
/*RZ961206- Add time to date*/
DECLARE @RegisterTime NVARCHAR(8)
SET @RegisterTime = (CONVERT(CHAR(8), @RegisterDate, 108))
/************************************ Error *************************************/
DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT, @IsDuplicate BIT /*AI14000201 - Added @IsDuplicate*/
SET @IsDuplicate = 0 /*AI14000201*/
/************************ Make A Table For ProjectList **************************/
DECLARE @ProjectTmpTable TABLE (
	[ProjectID] INT ,
	[prjCompanyId] INT ,
	[prjTitle] NVARCHAR(100) ,
	[prjDesc] NVARCHAR(300) ,
	[prjStartDate] NVARCHAR(8) ,

	[prjIsFinished] BIT ,

	[prjFirstRegisterDate] DATETIME ,
	[prjRemindDate] VARCHAR(8) ,

	/*RZ971103- Add field*/

	/*RZ971103- END*/
	/*AI14000131 - Add*/
	[prjHaveSms] BIT,
	[prjSmsPrsIds] NVARCHAR(3000),
	[prjSmsText] NVARCHAR(2000),
	[prjSmsSendDate] DATETIME,
	[prjSmsSendDatePersian] NVARCHAR(8),
	[prjInquiryNum] NVARCHAR(100),
	[prjSubject] NVARCHAR(1000),
	[prjTitleGroup] NVARCHAR(200),
	[prjCategoryId] INT,
	[prjDescI] NVARCHAR(MAX),
	[prjNumRef] NVARCHAR(150),
	[prjNumR] BIGINT,
	/*AI14000131 End*/
	/*AI14010317*/
	[prjToDoDateTime] DATETIME,
	[prjToDoRegisterDateTime] DATETIME,
	[prjToDoDoneDateTime] DATETIME,
	[prjFOCmpCanInsCnvs] BIT,
	[prjFOCmpCanNotInsCnvs] BIT,
	[prjTicketStop] BIT,
	[prjIsTicketNew] BIT,
	/*AI14010317-end*/
	[prjToDoTimeSec] BIGINT,/*AI14010412*/
	[prjToDoTimeRegisterHistory] NVARCHAR(MAX), /*AI14010416*/
	/*AI14010728*/
	[prjcmpPresentationId] INT,
	[prjcmpCampaignIds] NVARCHAR(2000),
	/*AI14010728-End*/
	[prjcmpQuickRegisterDefSettingId] INT, /*AI14020504*/
	/*AI14020718*/
	[prjValidityPrdId] INT,
	[prjValidityOrdItemId] INT
	/*AI14020718-End*/
)
/********************************************************************************/
/***************************** Split fields *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** Project *****************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRAN
/**/
DECLARE @PrjAllFields NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
DECLARE @PrjField NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
/**************** Define & set null field **************/
DECLARE @ProjectID INT = NULL,
	@prjCompanyId INT = NULL,
	@prjTitle NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
	@prjDesc NVARCHAR(300) = NULL,
	@prjStartDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@prjStartDay NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
	@prjStartTime NVARCHAR(15) = NULL,
	@prjStartDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
	@prjStarterUserId INT = NULL,
	@prjFinishedStatusId INT = NULL,
	@prjIsFinished BIT = NULL,
	@prjFinishedDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@prjFinishedDay NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@prjFinishedTime NVARCHAR(15) = NULL,
	@prjFinishedDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
	@prjFinishedUserId INT = NULL,
	@prjGradeId INT = NULL,
	@prjPriorityId INT = NULL,
	@prjFirstRegisterUserId INT = NULL,
	@prjGroupId INT = NULL,
	@prjcnvsStatusGroupId INT = NULL,
	@prjOrderView INT = NULL,
	@prjPostFix NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
	@prjFirstRegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@prjFirstRegisterDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@prjRemindDate VARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@prjRemindDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
	@prjInsertForbidden BIT = NULL,
	/*RZ971103- Add field*/ 	
	@prjFOCmpCanSee BIT = NULL,
	@prjIsTicket BIT = NULL,
	@prjUserDepartmentId INT = NULL,
	/*AI14000131 - Added*/
	@prjHaveSms BIT = NULL,
	@prjSmsPrsIds NVARCHAR(3000) = NULL,
	@prjSmsText NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,
	@prjSmsSendDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@prjSmsSendDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@prjInquiryNum NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
	@prjSubject NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
	@prjTitleGroup NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
	@prjCategoryId INT = NULL,
	@prjDescI NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@prjNumRef NVARCHAR(150) = NULL,
	@prjNumR BIGINT = NULL,
	/*AI14000131 End*/
	/*AI14010317*/
	@prjToDoDate NVARCHAR(8),
	@prjToDoTime NVARCHaR(15),
	@prjToDoDateTime DATETIME,
	@prjToDoRegisterDate NVARCHAR(8),
	@prjToDoRegisterTime NVARCHaR(15),
	@prjToDoRegisterDateTime DATETIME,
	@prjToDoDoneDate NVARCHAR(8),
	@prjToDoDoneTime NVARCHaR(15),
	@prjToDoDoneDateTime DATETIME,
	@prjFOCmpCanInsCnvs BIT,
	@prjFOCmpCanNotInsCnvs BIT,
	@prjTicketStop BIT,
	@prjIsTicketNew BIT,
	/*AI14010317-end*/
	@prjToDoTimeSec BIGINT, /*AI14010412*/
	@prjToDoTimeRegisterHistory NVARCHAR(MAX), /*AI14010416*/
	/*AI14010728*/
	@prjcmpPresentationId INT,
	@prjcmpCampaignIds NVARCHAR(2000),
	/*AI14010728-End*/
	@prjcmpQuickRegisterDefSettingId INT, /*AI14020504*/
	/*AI14020718*/
	@prjValidityPrdId INT,
	@prjValidityOrdItemId INT
	/*AI14020718-End*/

IF(@ProjectList IS NOT NULL AND @ProjectList <> '')
BEGIN	
WHILE LEN(@ProjectList) > 0 AND (@ProjectList IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	/*@ProjectList = N'prjTitle=title1|&|prjGroupId=1|&||^#^|prjTitle=title2|&|prjGroupId=2|&||^#^|'*/
    IF PATINDEX('%|^#^|%', @ProjectList) > 0
    BEGIN
        SET @PrjAllFields = SUBSTRING(@ProjectList,
                                    0,
                                    PATINDEX('%|^#^|%', @ProjectList))
        /*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
        /*For example => @PrjAllFields = prjTitle=title1|&|prjGroupId=1|&|*/
        /*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
        /*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill fields  --------------------------------*/
        /*BEGIN PROJETC*/
        DECLARE @PjAllFields NVARCHAR(MAX)
        SET @PjAllFields = @PrjAllFields
        
        /**************** set null field **************/
        SET @ProjectID  = NULL
		SET @prjCompanyId  = NULL
		SET @prjTitle  = NULL
		SET @prjDesc  = NULL
		SET @prjStartDate  = NULL
		SET @prjStartDay  = NULL
		SET @prjStartTime  = NULL
		SET @prjStartDateTime  = NULL
		SET @prjStarterUserId  = NULL
		SET @prjFinishedStatusId  = NULL
		SET @prjIsFinished  = NULL
		SET @prjFinishedDate  = NULL
		SET @prjFinishedDay  = NULL
		SET @prjFinishedTime  = NULL
		SET @prjFinishedDateTime  = NULL
		SET @prjFinishedUserId  = NULL
		SET @prjGradeId  = NULL
		SET @prjPriorityId  = NULL
		SET @prjFirstRegisterUserId  = NULL
		SET @prjGroupId  = NULL
		SET @prjcnvsStatusGroupId  = NULL
		SET @prjOrderView  = NULL
		SET @prjPostFix  = NULL
		SET @prjFirstRegisterDatePersian  = NULL
		SET @prjFirstRegisterDate  = NULL
		SET @prjRemindDate  = NULL
		SET @prjRemindDateTime  = NULL
		SET @prjInsertForbidden  = NULL
		/*RZ971103- Add field*/ 	
		SET @prjFOCmpCanSee = NULL
		SET @prjIsTicket = NULL
		SET @prjUserDepartmentId = NULL
		/*RZ971103- END*/						
		/*AI14000131 - Added*/
		SET @prjHaveSms = NULL
		SET @prjSmsPrsIds = NULL
		SET @prjSmsText = NULL
		SET @prjSmsSendDate = NULL
		SET @prjSmsSendDatePersian = NULL
		SET @prjInquiryNum = NULL
		SET @prjSubject = NULL
		SET @prjTitleGroup = NULL
		SET @prjCategoryId = NULL
		SET @prjDescI = NULL
		SET @prjNumRef = NULL
		SET @prjNumR = NULL
		/*AI14000131 End*/ 
		/*AI14010317*/
		SET @prjToDoDate = NULL
		SET @prjToDoTime = NULL
		SET @prjToDoDateTime = NULL
		SET @prjToDoRegisterDate = NULL
		SET @prjToDoRegisterTime = NULL
		SET @prjToDoRegisterDateTime = NULL
		SET @prjToDoDoneDate = NULL
		SET @prjToDoDoneTime = NULL
		SET @prjToDoDoneDateTime = NULL
		SET @prjFOCmpCanInsCnvs = NULL
		SET @prjFOCmpCanNotInsCnvs = NULL
		SET @prjTicketStop = NULL
		SET @prjIsTicketNew = NULL
		/*AI14010317-end*/
		SET @prjToDoTimeSec = NULL /*AI14010412*/
		SET @prjToDoTimeRegisterHistory = NULL /*AI14010416*/
		/*AI14010728*/
		SET @prjcmpPresentationId = NULL
		SET @prjcmpCampaignIds = NULL
		/*AI14010728-End*/
		SET @prjcmpQuickRegisterDefSettingId = NULL/*AI14020504*/
		/*AI14020718*/
		SET @prjValidityPrdId = NULL
		SET @prjValidityOrdItemId = NULL
		/*AI14020718-End*/
        /**/
        WHILE LEN(@PjAllFields) > 0
		BEGIN
			IF PATINDEX('%|#|%', @PjAllFields) > 0
			BEGIN
				SET @PrjField = SUBSTRING(@PjAllFields,
											0,
											PATINDEX('%|#|%', @PjAllFields))
				/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*Get parameter name and split value form parameter*/
				/*For example => prjGroupId=102 => prjGroupId= => prjGroupId => Compare => value*/
				DECLARE @PrjFieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
				DECLARE @PrjFieldName NVARCHAR(200)
				SET @PrjFieldName = SUBSTRING(@PrjField, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@PrjField)-1)                                    
				SET @PrjFieldValue = SUBSTRING(@PrjField, CHARINDEX('=', @PrjField)+1, Len(@PrjField))
				/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill variabele ------------------------------*/
				/*BEGIN Project*/
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrjFieldName = 'ProjectID'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @ProjectID = CASE WHEN @PrjFieldValue= 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrjFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrjFieldName = 'prjCompanyId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @prjCompanyId = CASE WHEN @PrjFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrjFieldValue AS INT) END

					/*RZ971222- Add check access to cmp */
					IF(@prjCompanyId IS NOT NULL)
					BEGIN
						DECLARE @IsAccessCompanyId BIT = 0

						EXEC dbo.MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser 	
						@Mode = 8,
						@Id = @prjCompanyId,
						@CrmUserId = @UserId,
						@IsAccess = @IsAccessCompanyId OUTPUT
				
						IF(@IsAccessCompanyId = 0)
						BEGIN
							DECLARE @CmpName NVARCHAR(2000)
							SET @CmpName = (SELECT
												CASE  WHEN (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
													THEN (SELECT  
															ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId),'') + ' ' +
															ISNULL(cmpName,'')
													FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @prjCompanyId)
													ELSE (SELECT  
															ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' +
															ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CL WHERE CL.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CL.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
														  FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @prjCompanyId)
												END
											 )
							
							SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=CompanyAccess|' + CAST(@ProjectID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '|PRJID|' + ISNULL(@CmpName,'') + '|Msg=CompanyAccess|'
						END	 
					END
					/*RZ971222- END*/
				END

				,@CantSaveDupPrjNumRByAllPrj_I = [IsActivePrjNumRByAllPrj]
			FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting]

			IF(@CantSaveDupPrjNumRByCmpPrj_I = 1)
			BEGIN
				SET @prjNumR =(SELECT [dbo].[MobfnReturnProjectNumR](@prjCompanyId,''))					
			END
			ELSE IF(@CantSaveDupPrjNumRByAllPrj_I = 1)

		/**************NumRef Check Dup****************/
		IF EXISTS (
		SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
		WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmDefineProjects' )
		AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actAutoCalcNumRef'
							AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmDefineProjects' ))
		AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
		BEGIN	
			EXEC dbo.MobspReturnProjectNumRef
				@prjCompanyId = @prjCompanyId,
				@prjInquiryNum = @prjInquiryNum,
				@prjNumR = @prjNumR,
				@prjStarterUserId = @prjStarterUserId,
				@languageID = @LanguageID,
				@defaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,
				@selectedNumRef = @prjNumRef OUTPUT
		END

		IF(ISNULL(@prjNumRef, '') <> '')
		BEGIN
			IF EXISTS (
			SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
			WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmDefineProjects' )
			AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actNotDupNumRef'
								AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmDefineProjects' ))
			AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/)
			AND @prjNumRef IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
				EXEC MobspCheckDuplicatePrjNumRef
					@prjNumRef = @prjNumRef,
					@CurrId = 0,
					@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
					@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

				IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
				BEGIN
					SET @IsDuplicate = 1
				END
			END
		END

		END
		/**************NumR Check Dup****************/
		IF(ISNULL(@prjNumR, 0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			DECLARE @CantSaveDupPrjNumRByCmpPrj BIT
			DECLARE @CantSaveDupPrjNumRByAllPrj BIT

			SELECT TOP 1 
				@CantSaveDupPrjNumRByCmpPrj = [IsActivePrjNumRByCmpPrj]
				,@CantSaveDupPrjNumRByAllPrj = [IsActivePrjNumRByAllPrj]
			FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting]


			IF(@CantSaveDupPrjNumRByCmpPrj = 1)
			BEGIN
				EXEC [MobspCheckDuplicatePrjNumR]
					@CompanyId = @prjCompanyId,
					@prjNumR = @prjNumR,
					@CurrId = 0,
					@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
					@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

				IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
				BEGIN
					SET @IsDuplicate = 1
				END
			END
			ELSE IF(@CantSaveDupPrjNumRByAllPrj = 1)
			BEGIN
				EXEC [MobspCheckDuplicatePrjNumR]
					@CompanyId = 0,
					@prjNumR = @prjNumR,
					@CurrId = 0,
					@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
					@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

				IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
				BEGIN
					SET @IsDuplicate = 1
				END
			END
		END
		/******************************/ /*AI14020718 - Add check*/
		IF(ISNULL(@prjValidityPrdId, 0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@prjValidityOrdItemId, 0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			DECLARE @SelectedValidity INT = NULL
			EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckProjectValidityOrdItem]
				@OrderItemId = @prjValidityOrdItemId,
				@CurrentDate = @RegisterDate,
				@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
				@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,
				@SelectedValidity = @SelectedValidity OUTPUT

			IF(ISNULL(@SelectedValidity, 0) = 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'Msg=8'
			END
		END


		/******************************/
		INSERT INTO @ProjectTmpTable 
			(
				[ProjectID]  ,
				[prjCompanyId]  ,
				[prjTitle]  ,
				[prjDesc]  ,
				[prjStartDate]  ,
				[prjStartDay]  ,
				[prjStartTime]  ,
				[prjStartDateTime]  ,
				[prjStarterUserId]  ,
				[prjFinishedStatusId]  ,
				[prjIsFinished]  ,
				[prjFinishedDate]  ,
				[prjFinishedDay]  ,
				[prjFinishedTime]  ,
				[prjFinishedDateTime]  ,
				[prjFinishedUserId]  ,
				[prjGradeId]  ,
				[prjPriorityId]  ,
				[prjFirstRegisterUserId]  ,
				[prjGroupId]  ,
				[prjcnvsStatusGroupId]  ,
				[prjOrderView]  ,
				[prjPostFix]  ,
				[prjFirstRegisterDatePersian]  ,
				[prjFirstRegisterDate]  ,
				[prjRemindDate]  ,
				[prjRemindDateTime]  ,
				[prjInsertForbidden] ,
				/*RZ971103- Add field*/ 	
				[prjFOCmpCanSee] ,
				[prjIsTicket] ,
				[prjUserDepartmentId],
				/*RZ971103- END*/ 
				/*AI14000131 - Add*/
				[prjHaveSms],
				[prjSmsPrsIds],
				[prjSmsText],
				[prjSmsSendDate] ,
				[prjSmsSendDatePersian],
				[prjInquiryNum] ,
				[prjSubject] ,
				[prjTitleGroup] ,
				[prjCategoryId] ,
				[prjDescI] ,
				[prjNumRef] ,
				[prjNumR],
				/*AI14000131 End*/ 
				/*AI140100317*/
				[prjToDoDateTime],
				[prjToDoRegisterDateTime],
				[prjToDoDoneDateTime],
				[prjFOCmpCanInsCnvs],
				[prjFOCmpCanNotInsCnvs],
				[prjTicketStop],
				[prjIsTicketNew],
				/*AI140100317-End*/
				[prjToDoTimeSec], /*AI14010412*/
				[prjToDoTimeRegisterHistory], /*AI14010416*/
				/*AI14010728*/
				[prjcmpPresentationId],
				[prjcmpCampaignIds],
				/*AI14010728-End*/
				[prjcmpQuickRegisterDefSettingId], /*AI14020504*/
				/*AI14020718*/
				[prjValidityPrdId],
				[prjValidityOrdItemId]
				/*AI14020718-End*/
			)
			VALUES
			(
				@ProjectID  ,
				@prjCompanyId  ,
				@prjTitle  ,
				@prjDesc  ,
				@prjStartDate  ,
				@prjStartDay  ,
				@prjStartTime  ,
				@prjStartDateTime  ,
				@prjStarterUserId  ,
				@prjFinishedStatusId  ,
				@prjIsFinished  ,
				@prjFinishedDate  ,
				@prjFinishedDay  ,
				@prjFinishedTime  ,
				@prjFinishedDateTime  ,
				@prjFinishedUserId  ,
				@prjGradeId  ,
				@prjPriorityId  ,
				@prjFirstRegisterUserId  ,
				@prjGroupId  ,
				@prjcnvsStatusGroupId  ,
				@prjOrderView  ,
				@prjPostFix  ,
				@prjFirstRegisterDatePersian  ,
				@prjFirstRegisterDate  ,
				@prjRemindDate  ,
				@prjRemindDateTime  ,
				@prjInsertForbidden  ,
				/*RZ971103- Add field*/ 	
				@prjFOCmpCanSee ,
				@prjIsTicket ,
				@prjUserDepartmentId,
				/*RZ971103- END*/
				/*AI14000131 - Added*/
				@prjHaveSms,
				@prjSmsPrsIds,
				@prjSmsText ,
				@prjSmsSendDate ,
				@prjSmsSendDatePersian ,
				@prjInquiryNum,
				@prjSubject,
				@prjTitleGroup,
				@prjCategoryId,
				@prjDescI,
				@prjNumRef,
				@prjNumR,
				/*AI14000131 End*/ 
				/*AI140100317*/
				@prjToDoDateTime,
				@prjToDoRegisterDateTime,
				@prjToDoDoneDateTime,
				@prjFOCmpCanInsCnvs,
				@prjFOCmpCanNotInsCnvs,
				@prjTicketStop,
				@prjIsTicketNew,
				/*AI140100317-End*/
				@prjToDoTimeSec, /*AI14010412*/
				@prjToDoTimeRegisterHistory, /*AI14010416*/
				/*AI14010728*/
				@prjcmpPresentationId,
				@prjcmpCampaignIds,
				/*AI14010728-End*/
				@prjcmpQuickRegisterDefSettingId, /*AI14020504*/
				/*AI14020718*/
				@prjValidityPrdId,
				@prjValidityOrdItemId
				/*AI14020718-End*/
			) 										                      
		/*END PROJETC*/		
        /*END GENERAL*/
        /*---------------------------- END Fill fields ------------------------------------*/
        /*---------------------------- Remove current parameter from listparam ------------*/
        SET @ProjectList = SUBSTRING(@ProjectList,
                                  LEN(@PrjAllFields + '|^#^|') + 1,
                                  LEN(@ProjectList))                                  
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SET @PrjAllFields = @ProjectList
        SET @ProjectList = NULL
        
    END
END
END
ELSE
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=4' 	
/**/
IF (@@ERROR > 0)
BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Input Project' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
ELSE 
COMMIT TRAN

					/*RZ971103- END*/
					/*AI14010317*/,@prjToDoDateTime, @prjToDoRegisterDateTime, @prjToDoDoneDateTime, @prjFOCmpCanInsCnvs, @prjFOCmpCanNotInsCnvs, @prjTicketStop, @prjIsTicketNew, /*AI14010317*/
					@prjToDoTimeSec /*AI14010412*/, @prjToDoTimeRegisterHistory /*AI14010416*/, @prjcmpPresentationId, @prjcmpCampaignIds, /*AI14010728*/
					@prjcmpQuickRegisterDefSettingId /*AI14020504*/ /*AI14020718*/,@prjValidityPrdId, @prjValidityOrdItemId /*AI14020718-End*/
			WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0

					IF(ISNULL(@prjIsTicket, 0) = 1 AND ISNULL(@prjIsFinished, 0) = 1)

				ELSE /*when companyid <> @prjCompanyId that come from mobile*/
				BEGIN
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '<PRJID>' + CAST(@ProjectID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '<\PRJID>'  + 'Msg=1'																		 
				END

			FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrPrj INTO @ProjectID, @prjCompanyId, @prjTitle, @prjDesc, @prjStartDate, @prjStartDay, @prjStartTime,

					@prjFOCmpCanSee, @prjIsTicket, @prjUserDepartmentId,@prjHaveSms,@prjSmsPrsIds,@prjSmsText ,@prjSmsSendDate ,

					/*AI14010317*/,@prjToDoDateTime, @prjToDoRegisterDateTime, @prjToDoDoneDateTime, @prjFOCmpCanInsCnvs, @prjFOCmpCanNotInsCnvs, @prjTicketStop, @prjIsTicketNew  /*AI14010317*/
					,@prjToDoTimeSec /*AI14010412*/, @prjToDoTimeRegisterHistory /*AI14010416*/, @prjcmpPresentationId, @prjcmpCampaignIds, /*AI14010728*/
					@prjcmpQuickRegisterDefSettingId /*AI14020504*/ /*AI14020718*/,@prjValidityPrdId, @prjValidityOrdItemId /*AI14020718-End*/
			END
			CLOSE CrsrPrj
			DEALLOCATE CrsrPrj   	
END

Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspIssueOrderFromPreOrder]
(		
/* 
	EXEC MobspIssueOrderFromPreOrder  18, '13890714',1, 1, 3, 7, 1, '13890714'
*/
@PreOrderID INT,
@OrderDate VARCHAR(8),
@OrderOprId INT,
@OrderPrintFormatID INT,
@OrderReferUserId INT,
@UserId INT,
@Month1 INT,
@FPId INT,
@RegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8),
@AppType NVARCHAR(50), 
/*950403*/
@ordNote NVARCHAR(4000)= NULL, 
@ordSettlementTypeId INT= NULL,
@OrderDefaultSettingId INT= NULL,  
/**/
@InsertedOrderID INT OUTPUT ,
@InsertedOrderNumber BIGINT OUTPUT,/*RZ990811- Change type from int to bigint*/
/*ADBF951130*/
@LanguageID INT,
/*RZ960218 for multi language And add length variable*/
@ordNoteLang NVARCHAR(MAX)= NULL,
/*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
@OrdProjectId INT = NULL,
@PordItemList NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
@IsTransferFreePay BIT = NULL,
@IsTransferFreeComm BIT = NULL,
/*RZ14010531- End*/
@ordCompanyPersonId INT = NULL,/*RZ14020531- Add person and issue orderindex*/
/*RZ14020604- Add parameters*/
@ordIssueSettingByPrdId INT = NULL,
@ordIssueIndexedByPrdId INT = NULL
/*RZ14020604- End*/
)
AS
BEGIN
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
IF(ISNULL(@OrdProjectId,0) = 0 )
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @OrdProjectId = (SELECT pordProjectId FROM dbo.WSPreOrder PO WHERE PO.PreOrderId = @PreOrderID)
/**/
END
/**/
IF(ISNULL(@PordItemList,'') = '')
BEGIN
/**/	
	/*---------------------------------------*/
	/*---------- Issue one order ------------*/
	SET @IsTransferFreePay = 1
	SET @IsTransferFreeComm = 1
	/*---------------------------------------*/
	IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) OrderId FROM WSOrder WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderID ),0) = 0) /*PreOrder not have any order*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @PordItemList = (SELECT 
								PreOrderItemID [Id],			
								pordIQuantity [Value],
								/*RZ14020604- Add parameters and expireDate in items*/
								pordIValidityExpireDate [ValidityExpireDate],
								pordIValidityExpireDatePersian [ValidityExpireDatePersian],
								pordIPrdAutoCalcRate [PrdAutoCalcRate]
								/*RZ14020604- End*/
							FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems
							WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderID		
							ORDER BY ISNULL(pordIOrderView, PreOrderItemID)	
							FOR JSON PATH)
	/**/
	END
	ELSE /*IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) OrderId FROM WSOrder WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderID ),0) > 0)*/ /*PreOrder have order*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @PordItemList = (SELECT 
								PreOrderItemID [Id],			
								(ISNULL(pordIQuantity,0) - 
								ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ordIQuantity) 
										FROM dbo.WSOrderItems OI
										WHERE OrderId IN(SELECT O.OrderId 
														 FROM dbo.WSOrder O 
														 WHERE O.PreOrderId = POI.PreOrderId
														 )
										/*AND ordIWHId = pordIWHId
										AND ordIPrdId = pordIPrdId
										AND ordIPrice = pordIPrice*/
										/*RZ14021202- Add PreOrderItemId to WSOrderItems*/
										AND OI.PreOrderItemId = POI.PreOrderItemID
										/*RZ14021202- End*/
														 ),0)
	
								) [Value],
								/*RZ14020604- Add parameters and expireDate in items*/
								pordIValidityExpireDate [ValidityExpireDate],
								pordIValidityExpireDatePersian [ValidityExpireDatePersian],
								pordIPrdAutoCalcRate [PrdAutoCalcRate]
								/*RZ14020604- End*/
							FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems POI
							WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderID								
							ORDER BY ISNULL(pordIOrderView, PreOrderItemID)	
							FOR JSON PATH)
		/*AI14020710- Add condition*/
		IF(ISNULL(@PordItemList,'') <>'')
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @PordItemList = (SELECT 
									 [Id]
									,[Value]
									,[ValidityExpireDate]
									,[ValidityExpireDatePersian]
									,[PrdAutoCalcRate]
								FROM OPENJSON(@PordItemList)
								WITH (
								[Id] INT '$.Id',
								[Value] FLOAT '$.Value'	,
								[ValidityExpireDate] DATETIME '$.ValidityExpireDate',
								[ValidityExpireDatePersian] NVARCHAR(8) '$.ValidityExpireDatePersian',
								[PrdAutoCalcRate] NUMERIC(18,2) '$.PrdAutoCalcRate'			
								) AS JS	
								WHERE ISNULL([Value],0) > 0	
								FOR JSON PATH)
		/**/
		END
		/*AI14020710- End*/
	/**/
	END	
/**/
END
/**/
DECLARE @cuUnitIsDecimal BIT
SET @cuUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT TOP 1 [IsDecimal]
						FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] 
						WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = (SELECT pordCurrencyUnitId 
												  FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] 
												  WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderID))
/*RZ14010531- End*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @InsertedOldPreOrderID INT
/*SELECT @InsertedOldPreOrderID = OrderID FROM dbo.WSOrder Ord WHERE Ord.PreOrderId = @PreOrderID
IF @InsertedOldPreOrderID > 0 RETURN -1*/
/*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
/*برای اینکه برای یک پیش فاکتور بیشتر از تعداد مجاز فاکتور صادر نشود  چک براساس مجموعه تعداد اقلام  انجام می شود.
 زیرا می توان فاکتور با تعداد دلخواه کالا از پیش فاکتور صادر کرد.*/
IF(ISNULL((
	ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL(pordIQuantity,0)) FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderID),0)
	-
    ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL(ordIQuantity,0)) FROM dbo.WSOrderItems OI WHERE OI.OrderId IN(SELECT O.OrderId FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] O WHERE O.PreOrderId = @PreOrderID)),0)
	),0) <= 0 /*AI14020710 - Change condition from = 0 to <= 0*/

	OR ISNULL(@PordItemList,'') = ''/*AI14020710- Add condition*/
)
BEGIN
	RETURN -1
END
/*RZ14010531- End*/

SET @TimeNow = CONVERT(TIME, GETDATE())
SET @OrdDateTime = @OrdDateTime + CAST(@TimeNow AS DATETIME)
/*RZ980703- End*/

/*AI14020705 Begin*/
DECLARE @ordDay NVARCHAR(10)
SET @ordDay = DATEPART(WEEKDAY, @OrdDateTime)

IF @ordSettlementTypeId = 0 SET @ordSettlementTypeId = NULL
IF @OrderDefaultSettingId = NULL SET @OrderDefaultSettingId = 0
/*AI14020705 End*/

DECLARE
/*@Title VARCHAR(200)*/@Title NVARCHAR(MAX), /*RZ960218 for multi language And add length variable*/
/*@Subject VARCHAR(300)*/@Subject NVARCHAR(MAX), /*RZ960218 for multi language And add length variable*/
@GuarantyId INT, 
@TaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2), @CalculateTax BIT, 
/*@SpecialWord VARCHAR(50)*/@SpecialWord NVARCHAR(MAX), /*RZ960218 for multi language And add length variable*/
/*@DescriptionI VARCHAR(300)*/@DescriptionI NVARCHAR(MAX), /*RZ960218 for multi language And add length variable*/
/*@DescriptionII VARCHAR(500)*/@DescriptionII NVARCHAR(MAX), /*RZ960218 for multi language And add length variable*/

/*------------ for get record in lang table ---------*/
/*RZ960218 for multi language And add length variable*/
@OrderSettingID INT,
@Note NVARCHAR(MAX)/*RZ960829- add length nvarchar to max*/
SELECT 
@Title = Title, @Subject = [Subject], @GuarantyId = GuarantyId, @TaxIndex =

DescriptionII,

 /*RZ960218 for multi language And add length variable*/
 ,@OrderSettingID = OrderSettingID, @Note = Note
FROM dbo.WSOrderDefaultSetting
/*950304
WHERE UserId = @UserId
*/
WHERE ReferUserId = @UserId

						AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmIssueOrderFromPreOrder' ))
AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
	SET @HavePermissionMovePordDescIIToOrd = 1

	SET @HavePermissionMovePordDescIIToOrd = 0

@PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexedOprId INT = NULL,
@PrdOrdIssueSetSetting BIT = NULL,

@ActivePrjNumRWithTitle BIT = NULL,

Declare @OrderId INT
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/*--********************MAINTRAN*****************/
/*----------------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14020531- Add person and issue orderindex*/
SET @InsertedOrderID = NULL
/*-------- Insert project for order ------------*/
IF(ISNULL(@ordIssueSettingByPrdId,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@OrdProjectId,0) = -1000)/*RZ14020604- Add parameters and expireDate in items*/
BEGIN
/**/
	/*----------------------------------------*/
	SET @ProductId = @ordIssueSettingByPrdId/*RZ14020604- Add parameters and expireDate in items*/
	/*------------- Set variables ------------*/
	SELECT TOP (1) 	   
		 @PrdOrdIssueSetSetting = [PrdOrdIssueSetSetting]
		,@PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleGroupId = [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleGroupId]
		,@PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleId = [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleId]
		,@PrdOrdIssuePrjPostFix = [PrdOrdIssuePrjPostFix]
		,@PrdOrdIssuePrjCnvsStatusGroupId = [PrdOrdIssuePrjCnvsStatusGroupId]
		,@PrdOrdIssuePrjRemindDay = [PrdOrdIssuePrjRemindDay]
		,@PrdOrdIssuePrjFOCmpCanSee = [PrdOrdIssuePrjFOCmpCanSee]
		,@PrdOrdIssuePrjIsTicket = [PrdOrdIssuePrjIsTicket]
		,@PrdOrdIssuePrjUsedType = [PrdOrdIssuePrjUsedType]	
		/*RZ14020611- Change sp and add fields*/
		,@PrdOrdIssueUpdateCmpTypeId = [PrdOrdIssueUpdateCmpTypeId]
		,@PrdOrdIssueUpdateCmpGroupII = [PrdOrdIssueUpdateCmpGroupII]
		,@PrdOrdIssueUpdateTaxCmpGroupIdII = [PrdOrdIssueUpdateTaxCmpGroupIdII]
		,@PrdOrdIssueUpdateNoTaxCmpGroupIdII = [PrdOrdIssueUpdateNoTaxCmpGroupIdII]
		,@PrdOrdIssueDateToCmpEffectiveDate = [PrdOrdIssueDateToCmpEffectiveDate]
		/*RZ14020611- End*/
	FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
	WHERE [ProductID] = @ProductId
	/*---------------------------*/
	SET @InsertedPrjId = NULL
	IF(ISNULL(@PrdOrdIssueSetSetting,0) = 1)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		/*----------------------------------------*/
		SELECT TOP(1)
			 @prjTitle = [ProjectTitle]
			,@prjGroupId = [ProjectTitleGroupId]
			,@prjcnvsStatusGroupId = ISNULL(@PrdOrdIssuePrjCnvsStatusGroupId,[ProjectTitleStatusGroupId])
			,@prjGradeId = [ProjectTitleGradeId]
			,@prjPriorityId = [ProjectTitlePriorityId]
			,@prjDesc = [ProjectTitleDesc]
			,@ActivePrjNumRWithTitle = [ActivePrjNumRWithTitle]
		FROM dbo.CrmProjectTitles PT
		WHERE [ProjectTitleID] = @PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleId

		SELECT TOP (1) 
			@prjTitleGroup = [ProjectTitleGroup]
			/*,@prjUserDepartmentId = [ProjectTitleGroupUserDepartmentId]*/				
		FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectTitleGroups]
		WHERE [ProjectTitleGroupId] = @PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleGroupId
		/*----------------------------*/
		SET @prjPostFix = @PrdOrdIssuePrjPostFix
		SET @prjFOCmpCanSee = @PrdOrdIssuePrjFOCmpCanSee
		SET @prjIsTicket = @PrdOrdIssuePrjIsTicket
		/*----------------------------*/
		IF(@PrdOrdIssuePrjRemindDay IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @prjRemindDateTime = DATEADD(DAY, ISNULL(@PrdOrdIssuePrjRemindDay,0), @OrdDateTime)				
			SET @prjRemindDateTime = DATEADD(dd, 0, DATEDIFF(dd, 0, @prjRemindDateTime))
			SET @prjRemindDate = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](@prjRemindDateTime))
		/**/
		END	
		/*------------------------------------------------------*/			
		SET @InsertedPrjId = (SELECT TOP 1 P.ProjectID 
								FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] P 
								WHERE P.prjCompanyId = @pordCompanyId 
								AND ISNULL(prjIsFinished,0) = 0
								AND (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST((ISNULL((REPLACE((ISNULL(prjTitleGroup,'') + ISNULL(prjTitle,'') + ISNULL(prjPostFix,'')),' ' ,'')),'')) AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
								= 
								(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST((ISNULL((REPLACE((ISNULL(@prjTitleGroup,'') + ISNULL(@prjTitle,'') + ISNULL(@prjPostFix,'')),' ' ,'')),'')) AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
							)
		IF(ISNULL(@PrdOrdIssuePrjUsedType,0) = 1)/*(Issue in an existing project)*/
		BEGIN
		/**/
			IF(ISNULL(@InsertedPrjId,0) = 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @InsertedPrjId = -1
			/**/
			END
		/**/
		END
		ELSE IF(ISNULL(@PrdOrdIssuePrjUsedType,0) = 2)/*(Issuing in a new project)*/

				SET @CmpChangeHistoryDesc = (SELECT TOP(1) [Desc] FROM @CmpChangeHistoryDescTbl_Lang T WHERE T.LanguageID = @LanguageID)
				SET @CmpChangeHistoryDesc = REPLACE((REPLACE(@CmpChangeHistoryDesc,N'{0}',@OldValueName)),N'{1}',@NewValueName)
										
				SET @CmpChangeHistory_Lang = ISNULL('[' + ((STUFF((SELECT N',{' + 
																N'"LanguageID":' + CAST(L.[LanguageID] AS NVARCHAR(100)) +
																N',"OldValue":"' + ISNULL(@OldValue,'') + N'"' +
																N',"NewValue":"' + ISNULL(@NewValue,'') + N'"' +																
																N',"Desc":"' +
																	ISNULL(REPLACE((REPLACE((SELECT TOP(1) [Desc] FROM @CmpChangeHistoryDescTbl_Lang T WHERE T.LanguageID = L.[LanguageID]),N'{0}',																		
																	ISNULL((CASE WHEN ISNULL(@oldcmpGroupIdII,0) = 0
																			THEN (SELECT TOP(1) [Txt] FROM @CmpChangeHistoryDescNoValTbl_Lang T WHERE T.LanguageID = L.[LanguageID])
																			ELSE (SELECT TOP(1) [GroupIIName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyGroupsII_Lang] G WHERE G.CompanyGroupIIID = @oldcmpGroupIdII AND G.LanguageID = L.[LanguageID])																		
																	END),'')
																	))																																				
																	,N'{1}',																		
																	ISNULL((CASE WHEN ISNULL(@newcmpGroupIdII,0) = 0
																			THEN (SELECT TOP(1) [Txt] FROM @CmpChangeHistoryDescNoValTbl_Lang T WHERE T.LanguageID = L.[LanguageID])
																			ELSE (SELECT TOP(1) [GroupIIName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyGroupsII_Lang] G WHERE G.CompanyGroupIIID = @newcmpGroupIdII AND G.LanguageID = L.[LanguageID])																		
																	END),'')
																		
																	),'')
															+ N'"}'					
															FROM [dbo].[Languages] L
															WHERE ISNULL(L.IsActive,0) = 1	
															AND L.[LanguageID] <> @LanguageID
															FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, ''))				
												+ ']'),'')					
				/*-----------*/
				EXEC [dbo].[MobspInsertCompanyChangeHistory] /*AI14020705*/
				@CompanyId = @pordCompanyId,
				@FieldName = @FieldName,
				@OldValue = @OldValue,
				@NewValue = @NewValue,
				@Desc = @CmpChangeHistoryDesc, 
				@CmpChangeHistory_Lang = @CmpChangeHistory_Lang, 	
				@UserId = @UserId,
				@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
				@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
				@AppType = @AppType,
				@DefaultLanguageID = @LanguageID,		
				@InsertedId = @InsertedCmpChangeHistoryId OUTPUT

			/**/
			END			
			/*----------------------------------*/
		/**/
		END
		/*--------- Update companyEffectiveDate -------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@PrdOrdIssueDateToCmpEffectiveDate,0) = 1)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			UPDATE [dbo].[CrmCompany]
			   SET [cmpEffectiveDatePersian] = @OrderDate
				  ,[cmpEffectiveDateTime] = CAST(@OrdDateTime AS DATE)
				  ,[UserId] = @UserId
				  ,[Year1] = @FPId
				  ,[Month1] = @Month1
				  ,[RegisterDate] = @RegisterDate
				  ,[RegisterDatePersian] = @RegisterDatePersian
				  ,[AppType] = @AppType				  		  
			 WHERE CompanyID = @pordCompanyId			 
		/**/
		END

	/**/
	END
