			pordIDiscount,
			pordIDiscountI,
			pordITotalPrice, 
			pordIDescription, 
			pordIDiscountPercent,
			pordIDiscountIPercent,
			pordIRound,
			pordIRoundPlus,
			pordIPrdUnitId,
			pordICurrencyUnitId,
			pordIFCR, 
			pordIFCRI,
			pordIOrderView,
			pordITax, 
			pordITaxIndex, 
			pordICalculateTax, 
			pordINotVisible, 
			pordINotInSum,
			pordIWHId
			/*ADBF990126*/
			, pordIPrdPriceGroupID
			,pordITracing /*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
			/*RZ990815- Add fields to orderItems and preorderItems*/
				,[pordIPriceSum]
				,[pordIDiscountSum]
				,[pordIDiscountISum]
				,[pordITaxSum]
			/*RZ990815- End*/
			/*RZ990916- Add fields and commission*/
			,[pordIDeliveryTermId],[pordIDeliveryDate],[pordIDeliveryDatePersian]
			/*RZ990916- End*/
			,[pordIQuantityTotal] /*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
			,[pordIPrdColorId]/*RZ14010612- Add prdColorId*/
			/*RZ14010620- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
			,[pordIPrdUnitIdI]
			,[pordIPrdUnitIRateResult]
			/*RZ14010620- End*/
			,[pordIPrdNickname] /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/	
			/*RZ14020324- Add new fields*/
			,[pordIDiscountDesc]
			,[pordIDeliveryTime]
			,[pordIDeliveryDay]
			,[pordIDeliveryEndDate]
			,[pordIDeliveryEndDatePersian]
			,[pordIDeliveryEndTime]
			,[pordIDeliveryEndDay] 			
			/*RZ14020324- End*/
			,[pordILinkAccWHIdStr]/*RZ14020427- Add pordILinkAccWHIdStr*/
			/*RZ14020510- Add fields*/
			,[pordIValidityExpireDate]
			,[pordIValidityExpireDatePersian]
			/*RZ14020528- Change PrdAutoBill*/	
			,[pordIPrdAutoCalcRate]
			,[pordIPrdAutoCalcInflationRateInfo]
			/*RZ14020528- End*/				
			,[pordIDescriptionI]
			/*RZ14020510- End*/
		FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems
		WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderID
		/*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
		AND PreOrderItemID IN(SELECT [Id]
								FROM OPENJSON(@PordItemList)
								WITH (
									[Id] INT '$.Id',
									[Value] FLOAT '$.Value'	
								) AS JS	
								/*WHERE ISNULL([Value],0) > 0*/)
		/*RZ14010531- End*/		
		ORDER BY ISNULL(pordIOrderView, PreOrderItemID)	

	OPEN Crsr_Add_Item
	FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Item INTO @PreOrderItemID, @pordIPrdId, @pordIQuantity, 
								@pordIQuantityDesc, @pordIPrice, @pordIDiscount, @pordIDiscountI,
								@pordITotalPrice, @pordIDescription, @pordIDiscountPercent, @pordIDiscountIPercent, 
								@pordIRound, @pordIRoundPlus, @pordIPrdUnitId, @pordICurrencyUnitId, @pordIFCR,
								@pordIFCRI, @pordIOrderView, @pordITax, @pordITaxIndex, @pordICalculateTax,
								@pordINotVisible, @pordINotInSum, @pordIWHId
								/*ADBF990126*/
								, @pordIPrdPriceGroupID,@pordITracing /*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
								,@pordIPriceSum,@pordIDiscountSum,@pordIDiscountISum,@pordITaxSum/*RZ990815- Add fields to orderItems and preorderItems*/								
								,@pordIDeliveryTermId,@pordIDeliveryDate,@pordIDeliveryDatePersian/*RZ990916- Add fields and commission*/
								,@pordIQuantityTotal /*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
								,@pordIPrdColorId /*RZ14010612- Add prdColorId*/
								,@pordIPrdUnitIdI,@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult/*RZ14010620- Add PrdUnitIdI*/	
								,@pordIPrdNickname /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
								/*RZ14020324- Add new fields*/
								,@pordIDiscountDesc,@pordIDeliveryTime,@pordIDeliveryDay,@pordIDeliveryEndDate
								,@pordIDeliveryEndDatePersian,@pordIDeliveryEndTime,@pordIDeliveryEndDay 			
								/*RZ14020324- End*/
								,@pordILinkAccWHIdStr /*RZ14020427- Add pordILinkAccWHIdStr*/
								/*RZ14020510- Add fields*/
								,@pordIValidityExpireDate,@pordIValidityExpireDatePersian
								,@pordIPrdAutoCalcRate,@pordIPrdAutoCalcInflationRateInfo/*RZ14020528- Change PrdAutoBill*/								
								,@pordIDescriptionI
								/*RZ14020510- End*/


	WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
	BEGIN
	/**/
		/*----------------------------------------*/
		/*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
		DECLARE @CurrQty FLOAT = NULL
		/*SET @CurrQty = (SELECT TOP(1) [Value]
						FROM OPENJSON(@PordItemList)
						WITH (
							[Id] INT '$.Id',
							[Value] FLOAT '$.Value'	
						) AS JS	
						WHERE [Id] = @PreOrderItemID)*/


		/*RZ14020604- Add parameters and expireDate in items*/
		DECLARE @jsValidityExpireDate DATETIME = NULL,@jsValidityExpireDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
		@jsPrdAutoCalcRate NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
		/**/
		SELECT TOP(1) 
			@CurrQty = [Value],
			@jsValidityExpireDate = [ValidityExpireDate],
			@jsValidityExpireDatePersian = [ValidityExpireDatePersian],
			@jsPrdAutoCalcRate = [PrdAutoCalcRate]
		FROM OPENJSON(@PordItemList)
		WITH (
			[Id] INT '$.Id',
			[Value] FLOAT '$.Value'	,
			[ValidityExpireDate] DATETIME '$.ValidityExpireDate',
			[ValidityExpireDatePersian] NVARCHAR(8) '$.ValidityExpireDatePersian',
			[PrdAutoCalcRate] NUMERIC(18,2) '$.PrdAutoCalcRate'			
		) AS JS	
		WHERE [Id] = @PreOrderItemID	
		/*-------------------------------------------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@HasActionCanChangeValidityExpireDate,0) = 1)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @pordIValidityExpireDate = @jsValidityExpireDate
			SET @pordIValidityExpireDatePersian = @jsValidityExpireDatePersian
		/**/
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@HasActionCanChangePrdAutoCalcRate,0) = 1)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @pordIPrdAutoCalcRate = @jsPrdAutoCalcRate
		/**/
		END
		/**/
		/*RZ14020604- End*/		

		/*----------------------------------------------------*/
		/*----------------- Calculate ------------------------*/
		DECLARE @ordIQuantity FLOAT = NULL,@ordIPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@ordITotalPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@ordIDiscount NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordIDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@ordIOrderView INT = NULL,@ordIFCR FLOAT = NULL,@ordITax NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@ordITaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL, @ordICalculateTax BIT = NULL, 
		@ordIFCRI FLOAT = NULL,@ordIRound NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordIRoundPlus NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@ordIPriceSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordIDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@ordITaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordIQuantityTotal FLOAT = NULL,
		@PordTmpI NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@TmpI NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TmpII NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ItemCount INT = NULL,
		@TmpIII NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TmpPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@ExtraCalcItem NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ExtraCalcItemPercent FLOAT = NULL
			

		/*----------------------------------------------------*/
		SET @ordIPrice = @pordIPrice
		/*Set OrderView*/
		SET @ItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI WHERE OI.OrderId = @OrderId)
		SET @ordIOrderView = (@ItemCount + 1) + (@ItemCount * 4 ) - 1
		/**/
		SET @ordICalculateTax = @pordICalculateTax
		SET @ordIFCR = @pordIFCR
		SET @ordIFCRI = @pordIFCRI
		SET @ordIRound = @pordIRound
		SET @ordIRoundPlus = @pordIRoundPlus
		SET @ordITaxIndex = @pordITaxIndex
		/*------------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@CurrQty,0) = 0 )
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @ordIQuantity = @pordIQuantity
			SET @ordITotalPrice = @pordITotalPrice
			SET @ordIDiscount = @pordIDiscount
			SET @ordIDiscountPercent = @pordIDiscountPercent			
			SET @ordITax = @pordITax			
			SET @ordIPriceSum = @pordIPriceSum
			SET @ordIDiscountSum = @pordIDiscountSum
			SET @ordITaxSum = @pordITaxSum
			SET @ordIQuantityTotal = @pordIQuantityTotal 			
		/**/
		END
		ELSE IF(ISNULL(@CurrQty,0) <> 0 )
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @ordIQuantity = ISNULL(@CurrQty,0)
			IF(ISNULL(@pordIQuantityTotal,0) <> 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @ordIQuantityTotal = @ordIQuantity * ISNULL(@ordIFCR,1)
			END
			/**/
			SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1) * ISNULL(@ordIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@ordIFCRI,1)
			SET @PordTmpI = ISNULL(@pordIQuantity,1) * ISNULL(@pordIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@pordIFCRI,1)

			/*----------------------------------*/
			/*RZ14010620- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
			SET @PrdUnitIRate = NULL
			SET @PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = NULL
			SET @PrdQtyNoFCR = NULL			
			/*--------------*/			
			SELECT 				
				@PrdUnitIRate = [PrdUnitIRate],
				@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = [PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto],
				@PrdQtyNoFCR = [PrdQtyNoFCR]				
			FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
			WHERE ProductId = @pordIPrdId
			/*--------------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1) 
				SET @PordTmpI = ISNULL(@pordIQuantity,1) 
			/**/
			END
			/*----------------------------------------------------*/
			IF(/*ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto,0) = 1 AND*/ ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0) /*AI14020912- Calculate RateResult in fiexd PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto*/
			BEGIN
			/**/
				/*SET @pordIPrdUnitIRateResult = (1 / ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0)) * ISNULL(@TmpI,1)*/
				SET @pordIPrdUnitIRateResult = (1 / ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0)) * ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1)/*RZ14010628- Neutralize PrdQtyNoFCR and FRCS in PrdUnitIRateResult*/

				IF(ISNULL(@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @pordIPrdUnitIRateResult = CAST((FORMAT(@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult, '000.000')) AS FLOAT) 
				/**/
				END
			/**/
			END
			/*RZ14010620- End*/
			/*----------------------------------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@pordIDiscountSum,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @ExtraCalcItemPercent = (ISNULL(@pordIDiscountSum,0) / (ISNULL(@PordTmpI,1)))
				SET @ordIDiscountSum = ISNULL(@ExtraCalcItemPercent,0) * @TmpI
				SET @ordIDiscount = ISNULL(@ExtraCalcItemPercent,0)
				IF(ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) > 0)
					SET @ordIDiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@ordIDiscount,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0)
				/**/
				IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
				BEGIN					
					/*RZ14020626-Change Ceiling to Round*/
					SET @ordIDiscountSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordIDiscountSum))
					SET @ordIDiscount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordIDiscount))
					/*RZ14020626-End*/
				END
			/**/
			END
			/*-------------------------------------------------------*/
			SET @TmpPrice = ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) - ISNULL(@ordIDiscount,0) - ISNULL(@ordIRound,0) + ISNULL(@ordIRoundPlus,0)			
			SET @TmpIII = ((ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) - ISNULL(@ordIRound,0) + ISNULL(@ordIRoundPlus,0)) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0)) - ISNULL(@ordIDiscountSum,0)	
			/*-------------------------------------------------------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@ordICalculateTax,0) = 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @ordITaxSum = (ISNULL(@TmpIII,0) * ISNULL(@ordITaxIndex,0)) / 100
				SET @ordITax = (ISNULL(@TmpPrice,0) * ISNULL(@ordITaxIndex,0)) / 100	
				IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
				BEGIN	
					/*RZ14020626-Change Ceiling to Round*/
					SET @ordITaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordITaxSum))
					SET @ordITax = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordITax))
					/*RZ14020626- End*/
				END
			/**/
			END

			/*-------------------------------------------------------*/
			SET @ordIPriceSum = @TmpIII
			SET @ordITotalPrice = ISNULL(@ordIPriceSum,0) + ISNULL(@ordITaxSum,0)
			IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
			BEGIN			
				/*RZ14020626-Change Ceiling to Round*/
				SET @ordIPriceSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordIPriceSum))
				SET @cuUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@cuUnitIsDecimal))
				/*RZ14020626-End*/
			END 
		/**/
		END	
		/*----------------------------------------------------*/
		/*RZ14010531- End*/
		/*----------------------------------------*/

		/*RZ14010319- Add ordIValidityExpireDate*/
		DECLARE @ordIValidityExpireDate DATETIME = NULL,@ordIValidityExpireDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL
		,@PrdValidityDay INT = NULL,@PrdValidityTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,@IsCalcValidityExpireDate BIT = NULL
		,@PrdValidityTimeHour INT = NULL,@PrdValidityTimeMinute INT = NULL		
		SELECT  
				@PrdValidityDay = [PrdValidityDay]
			,@PrdValidityTime = [PrdValidityTime]      
		FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
		WHERE [ProductID] = @pordIPrdId
		AND ISNULL([PrdValidity],0) = 1

		IF((@PrdValidityDay IS NOT NULL))
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @IsCalcValidityExpireDate = 1
		/**/
		END

		IF(ISNULL(@PrdValidityTime,'') <> '')
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @IsCalcValidityExpireDate = 1
			SET @PrdValidityTimeHour = CAST((SUBSTRING(@PrdValidityTime,1,2)) AS INT)
			SET @PrdValidityTimeMinute = CAST((SUBSTRING(@PrdValidityTime,4,2)) AS INT)
		/**/
		END

		IF(ISNULL(@IsCalcValidityExpireDate,0) = 1)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @ordIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(DAY, ISNULL(@PrdValidityDay,0), @OrdDateTime)
			SET @ordIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(HOUR, ISNULL(@PrdValidityTimeHour,0), @ordIValidityExpireDate)
			SET @ordIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(MINUTE, ISNULL(@PrdValidityTimeMinute,0), @ordIValidityExpireDate)
			SET @ordIValidityExpireDatePersian = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](@ordIValidityExpireDate))
		/**/
		END		
		/*RZ14010319- End*/
		/*----------------------------------------*/
		/*RZ14020406- Add ordIPrdValidityCounting*/
		DECLARE @ordIPrdValidityCountingNum INT = NULL,@ordIPrdValidityCountingMin INT = NULL
		SELECT TOP (1) 					
				@ordIPrdValidityCountingNum = (CASE WHEN (ISNULL([PrdValidityCountingNumIsActive],0) = 1)
												THEN [PrdValidityCountingNum]
												ELSE NULL
											END)
			,@ordIPrdValidityCountingMin = (CASE WHEN (ISNULL([PrdValidityCountingMinIsActive],0) = 1)
												THEN [PrdValidityCountingMin]
												ELSE NULL
											END)
		FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
		WHERE [ProductID] = @pordIPrdId
		/*RZ14020406- End*/
		
		/*----------------------------------------*/
		INSERT INTO dbo.WSOrderItems (
			OrderId,
			ordIPrdId,
			ordIQuantity,
			ordIQuantityDesc,
			ordIPrice,
			ordIDiscount,
			ordIDiscountI,
			ordITotalPrice,
			ordIDescription,
			/*--*/
			ordIDiscountPercent,
			ordIDiscountIPercent,
			ordIRound,
			/*9512*/
			ordIRoundPlus,
			ordIPrdUnitId,
			ordICurrencyUnitId,
			ordIFCR, ordIFCRI,
			ordIOrderView,
			ordITax, 
			ordITaxIndex, 
			ordICalculateTax, 
			ordINotVisible, 
			ordINotInSum,
			UserId,	
			FPId, 
			Month1, 
			RegisterDate,
			RegisterDatePersian, 
			AppType, 
			ordIWHId,	
			/*ADBF951130*/	
			LanguageID,
			FirstRegisterDatePersian,
			FirstRegisterUserId,
			FirstRegisterDate 
			/*ADBF990126*/
			, ordIPrdPriceGroupID
			,ordITracing /*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
			/*RZ990815- Add fields to orderItems and preorderItems*/
			,[ordIPriceSum]
			,[ordIDiscountISum]
			,[ordIDiscountSum]
			,[ordITaxSum]
			/*RZ990815- End*/
			/*RZ990916- Add fields and commission*/
			,[ordIDeliveryTermId]
			,[ordIDeliveryDate]
			,[ordIDeliveryDatePersian]
			/*RZ990916- End*/
			/*RZ14010319- Add ordIValidityExpireDate*/
			/*,[ordIValidityExpireDate]
			,[ordIValidityExpireDatePersian]*/		/*RZ14020510*/
			/*RZ14010319- End*/
			,[ordIQuantityTotal] /*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
			,[ordIPrdColorId] /*RZ14010612- Add prdColorId*/
			/*RZ14010620- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
			,[ordIPrdUnitIdI]
			,[ordIPrdUnitIRateResult]
			/*RZ14010620- End*/	
			,[ordIPrdNickname] /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
			/*RZ14020324- Add new fields*/
			,[ordIDiscountDesc]
			,[ordIDeliveryTime]
			,[ordIDeliveryDay]
			,[ordIDeliveryEndDate]
			,[ordIDeliveryEndDatePersian]
			,[ordIDeliveryEndTime]
			,[ordIDeliveryEndDay] 			
			/*RZ14020324- End*/
			/*RZ14020406- Add ordIPrdValidityCounting*/
			,[ordIPrdValidityCountingNum] 
			,[ordIPrdValidityCountingMin]
			/*RZ14020406- End*/
			,[ordILinkAccWHIdStr] /*RZ14020427- Add pordILinkAccWHIdStr*/
			/*RZ14020510- Add fields*/
			,[ordIValidityExpireDate]
			,[ordIValidityExpireDatePersian]			
			/*RZ14020528- Change PrdAutoBill*/	
			,[ordIPrdAutoCalcRate]
			,[ordIPrdAutoCalcInflationRateInfo]
			/*RZ14020528- End*/	
			,[ordIDescriptionI]
			/*RZ14020510- End*/
			,[PreOrderItemId]/*RZ14021202- Add PreOrderItemId to WSOrderItems*/	
		) 
		VALUES( 
			@OrderId,
			@pordIPrdId, 
			@ordIQuantity,/*@pordIQuantity*/ /*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			@pordIQuantityDesc,
			@ordIPrice, /*@pordIPrice*//*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			@ordIDiscount,/*@pordIDiscount*/ /*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			@pordIDiscountI,
			@ordITotalPrice,/*@pordITotalPrice*//*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/ 
			@pordIDescription, 
			/*--*/
			@ordIDiscountPercent,/*@pordIDiscountPercent*//*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			@pordIDiscountIPercent,
			@ordIRound,/*@pordIRound*//*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			/*9512*/
			@ordIRoundPlus,/*@pordIRoundPlus*//*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			@pordIPrdUnitId,
			@pordICurrencyUnitId,
			@ordIFCR,/*@pordIFCR*/ /*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			@ordIFCRI,/*@pordIFCRI*//*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			@ordIOrderView,/*@pordIOrderView*//*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			@ordITax, /*@pordITax*//*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			@ordITaxIndex,/*@pordITaxIndex*/ /*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			@ordICalculateTax,/*@pordICalculateTax*/ /*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			@pordINotVisible, 
			@pordINotInSum,
			@UserId, 
			@FPId,
			@Month1, 
			GETDATE(), 
			@RegisterDatePersian, 
			@AppType, 
			@pordIWHId,
			/*ADBF951130*/	
			@LanguageID,
			@RegisterDatePersian,
			@UserId,
			GETDATE()
			/*ADBF990126*/
			, @pordIPrdPriceGroupID
			,@pordITracing /*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
			/*RZ990815- Add fields to orderItems and preorderItems*/
			,@ordIPriceSum /*@pordIPriceSum*/ /*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			,@pordIDiscountISum
			,@ordIDiscountSum/*@pordIDiscountSum*//*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			,@ordITaxSum /*@pordITaxSum*//*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			/*RZ990815- End*/
			/*RZ990916- Add fields and commission*/
			,@pordIDeliveryTermId
			,@pordIDeliveryDate
			,@pordIDeliveryDatePersian
			/*RZ990916- End*/
			/*RZ14010319- Add ordIValidityExpireDate*/
			/*,@ordIValidityExpireDate
			,@ordIValidityExpireDatePersian	*/ /*RZ14020510*/
			/*RZ14010319- End*/
			,@ordIQuantityTotal /*@pordIQuantityTotal*//*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*//*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			,@pordIPrdColorId /*RZ14010612- Add prdColorId*/
			/*RZ14010620- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
			,@pordIPrdUnitIdI
			,@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult
			/*RZ14010620- End*/
			,@pordIPrdNickname /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
			/*RZ14020324- Add new fields*/
			,@pordIDiscountDesc
			,@pordIDeliveryTime
			,@pordIDeliveryDay
			,@pordIDeliveryEndDate

	BEGIN TRAN
	/**/
	DECLARE @ordTotalAmount BIGINT 
	SET @ordTotalAmount = 
	(SELECT ISNULL(ordTotalAmount, 0) FROM dbo.WSOrder
	WHERE OrderID = @InsertedOrderID AND ordStatusId <> 4) 
	/**/
	DECLARE @RemainordAmount BIGINT 
	SET @RemainordAmount = 
	(SELECT ISNULL(ordTotalAmount, 0) FROM dbo.WSOrder
	WHERE OrderID = @InsertedOrderID AND ordStatusId <> 4)
	-
	(SELECT ISNULL( SUM(ordpayAmount), 0) sumpayAmount
	FROM dbo.WSOrderPayments
	WHERE ordpayOrderId = @InsertedOrderID)
	/**/
	IF @RemainordAmount = 0
	BEGIN 
	UPDATE WSOrder SET ordStatusId = 3	/*تسویه شده*/
	WHERE OrderID = @InsertedOrderID
	END
	ELSE
	IF (@RemainordAmount > 0  AND @RemainordAmount <  @ordTotalAmount)
	BEGIN
	UPDATE WSOrder SET ordStatusId = 2	/*کامل تسویه نشده*/
	WHERE OrderID = @InsertedOrderID
	END
	ELSE
	IF @RemainordAmount =  @ordTotalAmount
	BEGIN
	UPDATE WSOrder SET ordStatusId = 1	/*تسویه نشده*/
	WHERE OrderID = @InsertedOrderID
	END
	/**/
	IF (@@ERROR > 0)
	BEGIN 
		SET @ErrorFrom='UPDATE ordStatusId ' 
		SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 
	ROLLBACK TRAN 
	END		
	ELSE 
	COMMIT TRAN

		SET @PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdId = NULL
		SET @PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexed = NULL
		SET @PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexedOprId = NULL
		SET @ProductId = NULL
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
		/*----------- Find order index setting ---------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@ordIssueIndexedByPrdId,0) <> 0) /*RZ14020604- Add parameters and expireDate in items*/
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*----------------------------------------------*/
			SET @ProductId = @ordIssueIndexedByPrdId
			SELECT TOP(1) 
				 @ProductId = ProductID
				,@PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdId = [PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdId]
				,@PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexed = [PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexed]
				,@PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexedOprId = [PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexedOprId]
			FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] P
			WHERE ProductID = @ProductId
			AND ISNULL([PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexed],0) = 1
			/*----------------------------------------------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@ProductId,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexed,0) = 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				/*----------------------------------------------*/
				DECLARE @IndexedOrdId INT = NULL,@IndexedOrdDailyCounter BIGINT = NULL,
				@IndexedOrdNumber BIGINT = NULL			
				/*----------------------------------------------*/
				SET @IndexedOrdDailyCounter = (SELECT [dbo].[WSfnReturnOrderDailyCounter](@PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexedOprId, @OrdDateTime))
				SET @IndexedOrdNumber =  (SELECT [dbo].[WSfnReturnOrderNumber](@FPId, @PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexedOprId))
				/*----------------------------------------------*/
				SET @ordIsVerified = NULL
				SET @ordVerifiedRegisterDate = NULL
				SET @ordVerifiedRegisterDatePersian = NULL
				SET @ordVerifierId  = NULL
				SET @VerifyAfterIssue = NULL
				/**/
				SET @VerifyAfterIssue = (SELECT TOP(1) [VerifyAfterIssue] FROM [dbo].[WSOrderOpr] WHERE OrderOprId = @PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexedOprId)
				IF (ISNULL(@VerifyAfterIssue,0) = 1)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @ordIsVerified = 1
					SET @ordVerifiedRegisterDate = GETDATE()
					SET @ordVerifiedRegisterDatePersian = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](CAST(@ordVerifiedRegisterDate AS DATE)))
					SET @ordVerifierId = @UserId
				/**/
				END
				/*----------------------------------------------*/
				/*------------------- tran6 ----------------------*/
				BEGIN TRAN			   
					/*------------------------------------*/							
					INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrder]
						([OrderOprID]
						,[PreOrderId]
						,[PreOrderVerifierId]
						,[ordTitle]
						,[ordNumber]
						,[ordDate]
						,[ordCompanyId]
						,[ordCompanyPersonId]
						,[ordProjectId]
						,[ordSubject]
						,[ordTaxIndex]
						,[ordCalculateTax]
						,[ordIsVerified]
						,[ordVerifiedRegisterDate]
						,[ordVerifiedRegisterDatePersian]
						,[ordStatusId]
						,[ordIsFixed]
						,[ordGuarantyId]
						,[ordSpecialWord]
						,[ordDescriptionI]
						,[ordDescriptionII]
						,[ordDescriptionId]
						,[ordPrintFormatId]
						,[ordRLogoId]
						,[ordLLogoId]
						,[ordSigningI]
						,[ordSigningII]
						,[ordSigningIII]
						,[ordAccountNumberI]
						,[ordAccountShabaNumberI]
						,[ordAccountNumberII]
						,[ordAccountShabaNumberII]
						,[ordSignatureId]
						,[ordImportDate]
						,[ordFirstRegisterUserId]
						,[UserId]
						,[FPId]
						,[Month1]
						,[RegisterDate]
						,[RegisterDatePersian]
						,[ordVerifierId]
						,[ordSettlementTypeId]
						,[ordDay]
						,[ordTime]
						,[ordReferUserId]
						,[ordDiscountTitle]
						,[ordNote]
						,[ordcmpTitle]
						,[ordDateTime]
						,[ordImportDateTime]
						,[ordDeliveryDate]
						,[ordDeliveryDateTime]
						,[ordDeliveryTime]
						,[AppType]
						,[ordVerifierRequestId]
						,[LanguageID]
						,[FirstRegisterUserId]
						,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
						,[FirstRegisterDate]
						,[ordVerificationWorkFlowId]
						,[ordCurrencyUnitId]
						,[ordDeliveryTermId]
						,[ordDeliveryEndDate]
						,[ordDeliveryEndDateTime]
						,[ordDeliveryEndTime]
						,[ordDefWHId]
						,[ordDefPrdPriceGroupId]
						,[ordSellerId]
						,[ordEmailUsersInfoId]
						,[ordEmailUserDefaultSettingId]
						,[ordSaleCenterId]
						,[ordDefPayTypeId]
						,[ordDailyCounter]
						,[ordDeadlineDate]
						,[ordDeadlineDatePersian]
						,[ordTspSettingId]
						,[ordDefaultSettingId]
						,[ordDeliveryDay]
						,[ordDeliveryEndDay]
						/*RZ14030301- Add pordCurrencyUnitIdI and amounts*/
						,[ordCurrencyUnitIdI]
						,[ordCurrencyUnitIRate]
						/*RZ14030301- End*/
						)
					SELECT
						 @PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexedOprId
						,[PreOrderId]
						,[PreOrderVerifierId]
						,[ordTitle]
						,@IndexedOrdNumber
						,[ordDate]
						,[ordCompanyId]
						,[ordCompanyPersonId]
						,[ordProjectId]
						,[ordSubject]
						,[ordTaxIndex]
						,[ordCalculateTax]
						,@ordIsVerified
						,@ordVerifiedRegisterDate
						,@ordVerifiedRegisterDatePersian
						,1/*[ordStatusId]*/
						,1/*[ordIsFixed]*/
						,[ordGuarantyId]
						,[ordSpecialWord]
						,[ordDescriptionI]
						,[ordDescriptionII]
						,[ordDescriptionId]
						,[ordPrintFormatId]
						,[ordRLogoId]
						,[ordLLogoId]
						,[ordSigningI]
						,[ordSigningII]
						,[ordSigningIII]
						,[ordAccountNumberI]
						,[ordAccountShabaNumberI]
						,[ordAccountNumberII]
						,[ordAccountShabaNumberII]
						,[ordSignatureId]
						,[ordImportDate]
						,[ordFirstRegisterUserId]
						,[UserId]
						,[FPId]
						,[Month1]
						,[RegisterDate]
						,[RegisterDatePersian]
						,@ordVerifierId
						,[ordSettlementTypeId]
						,[ordDay]
						,[ordTime]
						,[ordReferUserId]
						,[ordDiscountTitle]
						,[ordNote]
						,[ordcmpTitle]
						,[ordDateTime]
						,[ordImportDateTime]
						,[ordDeliveryDate]
						,[ordDeliveryDateTime]
						,[ordDeliveryTime]
						,[AppType]
						,[ordVerifierRequestId]
						,[LanguageID]
						,[FirstRegisterUserId]
						,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
						,[FirstRegisterDate]
						,[ordVerificationWorkFlowId]
						,[ordCurrencyUnitId]
						,[ordDeliveryTermId]
						,[ordDeliveryEndDate]
						,[ordDeliveryEndDateTime]
						,[ordDeliveryEndTime]
						,[ordDefWHId]
						,[ordDefPrdPriceGroupId]
						,[ordSellerId]
						,[ordEmailUsersInfoId]
						,[ordEmailUserDefaultSettingId]
						,[ordSaleCenterId]
						,[ordDefPayTypeId]
						,@IndexedOrdDailyCounter
						,[ordDeadlineDate]
						,[ordDeadlineDatePersian]
						,[ordTspSettingId]
						,[ordDefaultSettingId]
						,[ordDeliveryDay]
						,[ordDeliveryEndDay]
						/*RZ14030301- Add pordCurrencyUnitIdI and amounts*/
						,[ordCurrencyUnitIdI]
						,[ordCurrencyUnitIRate]
						/*RZ14030301- End*/
					FROM [dbo].[WSOrder]
					WHERE OrderID = @InsertedOrderID

					SET @IndexedOrdId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

					IF(ISNULL(@IndexedOrdId ,0) > 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						/*------------------------------------*/	
						/*------------ MultiLang -------------*/	
						INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrder_Lang]
							   ([OrderID]
							   ,[ordTitle]
							   ,[ordSubject]
							   ,[ordSpecialWord]
							   ,[ordDescriptionI]
							   ,[ordDescriptionII]
							   ,[ordSigningI]
							   ,[ordSigningII]
							   ,[ordSigningIII]
							   ,[ordDiscountTitle]
							   ,[ordNote]
							   ,[ordcmpTitle]
							   ,[LanguageID])
						 SELECT
								@IndexedOrdId
							   ,[ordTitle]
							   ,[ordSubject]
							   ,[ordSpecialWord]
							   ,[ordDescriptionI]
							   ,[ordDescriptionII]
							   ,[ordSigningI]
							   ,[ordSigningII]
							   ,[ordSigningIII]
							   ,[ordDiscountTitle]
							   ,[ordNote]
							   ,[ordcmpTitle]
							   ,[LanguageID]
							FROM [dbo].[WSOrder_Lang]
							WHERE [OrderID] = @InsertedOrderID
						/*------------------------------------*/	
						/*------------ OrderItem -------------*/
						DECLARE @IndexedOrderItemId INT = NULL,
						@IndexedOrdIPrdId INT = NULL,@IndexedOrdIQuantity FLOAT = NULL,
						@IndexedOrdICurrencyUnitId INT = NULL,@IndexedOrdIOrderView INT = NULL,
						@IndexedOrdIWHId INT = NULL,@IndexedOrdIPrdPriceGroupID INT = NULL,
						@IndexedOrdIPrdNickname NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
						@IndexedOrdIValidityExpireDate DATETIME = NULL,@IndexedOrdIValidityExpireDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
						@IndexedOrdIPrdAutoCalcRate NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@IndexedOrdIPrdAutoCalcInflationRateInfo NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
						@IndexedOrdItemCount INT = NULL
						/**/					
						/*------------------------------------*/
						SELECT TOP(1) 
							 @IndexedOrdICurrencyUnitId = ordCurrencyUnitId
							,@IndexedOrdIWHId = ordDefWHId
							,@IndexedOrdIPrdPriceGroupID = ordDefPrdPriceGroupId
						FROM [dbo].[WSOrder]
						WHERE OrderID = @IndexedOrdId
						/*---------*/
						IF(ISNULL(@IndexedOrdIWHId,0) = 0)
						BEGIN
						/**/
							SET @IndexedOrdIWHId = (SELECT TOP 1 [WareHouseID] FROM [dbo].[WsWareHouse] WHERE ISNULL(WrhsDeActive,0) = 0)
						/**/
						END
						/*--------*/
						IF(ISNULL(@IndexedOrdIPrdPriceGroupID,0) = 0)
						BEGIN
						/**/
							SET @IndexedOrdIPrdPriceGroupID = (SELECT TOP 1 ProductPriceGroupId FROM dbo.WSProductPriceGroup ORDER BY OrderView)
						/**/
						END
						/*--------*/
						SET @IndexedOrdIPrdId = @PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdId
						SET @IndexedOrdIQuantity = 1
						/*Set OrderView*/
						SET @IndexedOrdItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI WHERE OI.OrderId = @IndexedOrdId)
						SET @IndexedOrdIOrderView = (@IndexedOrdItemCount + 1) + (@IndexedOrdItemCount * 4 ) - 1
						/*-------*/
						SET @PrdValidityDay = NULL
						SET @PrdValidityTime = NULL
						SET @IsCalcValidityExpireDate = NULL
						SET @PrdValidityTimeHour = NULL
						SET @PrdValidityTimeMinute = NULL
						/**/
						SELECT  
							 @PrdValidityDay = [PrdValidityDay]
							,@PrdValidityTime = [PrdValidityTime] 
							,@IndexedOrdIPrdAutoCalcRate = [PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemRate]/*RZ14020621- Change fields name*/
							,@IndexedOrdIPrdAutoCalcInflationRateInfo = [PrdAutoCalcInflationLastDurationRate]/*RZ14020621- Change fields name*/
						FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
						WHERE [ProductID] = @IndexedOrdIPrdId
						AND ISNULL([PrdValidity],0) = 1

						IF((@PrdValidityDay IS NOT NULL))
						BEGIN
						/**/
							SET @IsCalcValidityExpireDate = 1
						/**/
						END

						IF(ISNULL(@PrdValidityTime,'') <> '')
						BEGIN
						/**/
							SET @IsCalcValidityExpireDate = 1
							SET @PrdValidityTimeHour = CAST((SUBSTRING(@PrdValidityTime,1,2)) AS INT)
							SET @PrdValidityTimeMinute = CAST((SUBSTRING(@PrdValidityTime,4,2)) AS INT)
						/**/
						END

						IF(ISNULL(@IsCalcValidityExpireDate,0) = 1)
						BEGIN
						/**/
							SET @IndexedOrdIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(DAY, ISNULL(@PrdValidityDay,0), @OrdDateTime)
							SET @IndexedOrdIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(HOUR, ISNULL(@PrdValidityTimeHour,0), @IndexedOrdIValidityExpireDate)
							SET @IndexedOrdIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(MINUTE, ISNULL(@PrdValidityTimeMinute,0), @IndexedOrdIValidityExpireDate)
							SET @IndexedOrdIValidityExpireDatePersian = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](@IndexedOrdIValidityExpireDate))
						/**/
						END		
						/*------------------------------------*/						
						INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderItems]
							([OrderId]
							,[ordIPrdId]
							,[ordIQuantity]
							,[ordIPrice]
							,[ordITotalPrice]
							,[UserId]
							,[FPId]
							,[Month1]
							,[RegisterDate]
							,[RegisterDatePersian]						
							,[ordICurrencyUnitId]
							,[ordIOrderView]						
							,[ordIWHId]
							,[AppType]
							,[LanguageID]
							,[FirstRegisterUserId]
							,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
							,[FirstRegisterDate]
							,[ordIPrdPriceGroupID]
							,[ordIPriceSum]						
							,[ordIValidityExpireDate]
							,[ordIValidityExpireDatePersian]
							,[ordIPrdNickname]						
							,[ordIPrdAutoCalcRate]
							,[ordIPrdAutoCalcInflationRateInfo])
						VALUES
							(@IndexedOrdId
							,@IndexedOrdIPrdId
							,@IndexedOrdIQuantity
							,0
							,0
							,@UserId
							,@FPId
							,@Month1
							,@RegisterDate
							,@RegisterDatePersian					
							,@IndexedOrdICurrencyUnitId
							,@IndexedOrdIOrderView						
							,@IndexedOrdIWHId
							,@AppType
							,@LanguageID
							,@UserId
							,@RegisterDatePersian
							,@RegisterDate
							,@IndexedOrdIPrdPriceGroupID
							,0						
							,@IndexedOrdIValidityExpireDate
							,@IndexedOrdIValidityExpireDatePersian
							,@IndexedOrdIPrdNickname						
							,@IndexedOrdIPrdAutoCalcRate
							,@IndexedOrdIPrdAutoCalcInflationRateInfo)
						SET @IndexedOrderItemId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
						IF(ISNULL(@IndexedOrderItemId,0) > 0 )
						BEGIN
						/**/
							INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderItems_Lang]
								([OrderItemID]
								,[LanguageID]
								,[ordIPrdNickname])
							SELECT 
								 @IndexedOrderItemId
								,[LanguageID]
								,[PrdNickname]
							FROM [dbo].[WSProducts_Lang]
							WHERE ProductID = @IndexedOrdIPrdId
						/**/
						ENd
						/*------------------------------------*/
						IF(ISNULL(@InsertedOrderID,0) > 0)
						BEGIN
						/**/
							EXEC [dbo].[MobspCalcAndUpdateOrderAmounts] /*AI14020705*/
							@OrderId = @IndexedOrdId,
							@Month1 = @Month1,
							@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
							@DefaultLanguageID = @LanguageID
						/**/
						END
						/*------------------------------------*/	
					/**/
					END
					/*------------------------------------*/		
				/*------------------- tran6 --------------------*/
				IF (@@ERROR > 0)
				BEGIN 
					SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into WSOrder For PrdIndexed' 
					SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 				
				ROLLBACK TRAN 
				END		
				ELSE 
				COMMIT TRAN 		
				/*----------------------------------------*/
				/*----------------------------------------------*/
			/**/
			END
			/*----------------------------------------------*/
		/**/
		END				
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
		/*---------------- OrdIndex END ----------------*/
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
		/*-------- Update prjRemindDate START ----------*/
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
		SET @ProductId = NULL
		SET @PrdValidityUpdatePrjRemindDateAfterIssueOrd = NULL
		SET @prjRemindDate = NULL
		SET @prjRemindDateTime = NULL
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
		/*------- Find product validty for update prjRemindDate ---------*/
		SELECT TOP(1) 
			 @ProductId = ProductID
			,@PrdValidityUpdatePrjRemindDateAfterIssueOrd = [PrdValidityUpdatePrjRemindDateAfterIssueOrd]			
		FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] P
		WHERE ProductID IN(SELECT TOP(1) pordIPrdId			
							FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems
							WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderID
							AND PreOrderItemID IN(SELECT [Id]
												FROM OPENJSON(@PordItemList)
												WITH (
													[Id] INT '$.Id',
													[Value] FLOAT '$.Value'	
												) AS JS)				  			
							ORDER BY ISNULL(pordIOrderView, PreOrderItemID))
		AND ISNULL([PrdValidityUpdatePrjRemindDateAfterIssueOrd],0) = 1
		AND ISNULL([PrdValidity],0) = 1
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@ProductId,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@PrdValidityUpdatePrjRemindDateAfterIssueOrd,0) =1)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SELECT TOP(1)
				 @prjRemindDateTime = ordIValidityExpireDate
				,@prjRemindDate = ordIValidityExpireDatePersian
			FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems]
			WHERE OrderId = @InsertedOrderID
			AND ordIPrdId = @ProductId
			AND ordIValidityExpireDate IS NOT NULL
			/**/
			IF(@prjRemindDateTime IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				UPDATE [dbo].[CrmProjects]
				SET prjRemindDateTime = @prjRemindDateTime
					,prjRemindDate = @prjRemindDate
				WHERE ProjectID = @OrdProjectId
			/**/
			END
		/**/
		END			   
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
		/*-------- Update prjRemindDate END ------------*/
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
	/**/
	END	
	/*RZ14020531- End*/
	/*----------------------------------------------*/
	/*----------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END

ELSE IF(ISNULL(@OrdProjectId,0) = 0)/*RZ14020531- Add person and issue orderindex*/
BEGIN
/**/
	RETURN -2
/**/
END
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*
--==============================================
--==============================================
--==============================================
--************/
IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
begin
	RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
	Set @OrderId = 0
	Return @OrderId
end
ELSE
BEGIN
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main
	Return @OrderId
END	
/*--****/
END









GO
-- [dbo].[MobspLetterIndicator] | References permission/temp-filter machinery






/*AI14020918 - Copied from web*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspLetterIndicator]
(	
	/* 
		EXEC MobspLetterIndicator  
			'1', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '' ,'' ,'' ,'3' ,'', ''
			,NULL ,NULL ,NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL, '', 1, 10, 1065, 1065
	*/
	@LetterTypeId VARCHAR(8),
	@LetterPreNumber VARCHAR(50), 
	@LetterNumber VARCHAR(8),
	@LetterNumberII VARCHAR(8),
	@LetterAnnex NVARCHAR(2000),
	@LetterAnnexLikely VARCHAR(8),
	@LetterCompanyNameId NVARCHAR(2000),
	@LetterSubject NVARCHAR(2000),
	@LetterSubjectLikely VARCHAR(8),
	@LetterHeader NVARCHAR(2000),
	@LetterHeaderLikely VARCHAR(8),
	@LetterContext NVARCHAR(2000),
	@LetterContextLikely VARCHAR(8),
	@LetterFooter NVARCHAR(2000),
	@LetterFooterLikely VARCHAR(8),
	@LetterRegUsersId VARCHAR(8),
	@LetterVerifierId VARCHAR(8),
	@LetterVerifyStatusId VARCHAR(8),
	@LetterPrintStatusId VARCHAR(8),
	@rptUserId VARCHAR(8) = NULL , 
	@rptDate VARCHAR(50) = NULL , 
	@rptDay VARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@LetterFDateTime DATETIME, 
	@LetterTDateTime DATETIME,
	@LetterRegFDateTime DATETIME,
	@LetterRegTDateTime DATETIME,
	@LetterPrintFDateTime DATETIME,
	@LetterPrintTDateTime DATETIME, 
	@LetterNumberCounterId VARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	/**/
	@PageNumber INT = NULL,
	@PageSize INT = NULL,
	@LanguageID INT = NULL,
	@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
As
BEGIN
	/**/
	DECLARE @FinalQry NVARCHAR(MAX) = '', @Qry NVARCHAR(MAX) = '', @From NVARCHAR(MAX) = '', @Term NVARCHAR(MAX) = '', @OrderBy VARCHAR(MAX) = ''
	/**/
	SET @From = '
		FROM dbo.SecLetters L 
		WHERE 1=1'
	SET	@OrderBy = ' ORDER BY LetterId DESC'
	/**/
	DECLARE @cmpDataPermision VARCHAR(MAX)
	EXEC [dbo].[CRMspSetcmpDataPermission] @rptUserId
	SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT
	FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
	WHERE TypeId = 20
	AND UserId = @rptUserId
	/*----------------------------------*/
	SET @Term = ' AND [FirstRegisterUserId] IN 
			(SELECT ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS VARCHAR(10)) + ' UNION 
			SELECT LinkedUserId FROM [dbo].[CrmLinkedUsers] 
			WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS VARCHAR(10)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 26 ) ' + 
			' AND L.LetterCompanyId IN (SELECT C.CompanyID FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] C WHERE 1 = 1 ' + ISNULL(@cmpDataPermision,'') + ' )'
	/**/
	IF ISNULL(@LetterTypeId, '') <> '' AND ISNULL(@LetterTypeId, '0') <> '0'	
	BEGIN 	
		SET @Term = @Term +
		' AND LetterTypeId = ' + @LetterTypeId 	
	END
	/**/
	IF (@LetterFDateTime IS NOT NULL AND @LetterTDateTime IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		SET @Term = @Term +
		' AND LetterDateTime BETWEEN ''' + CAST(@LetterFDateTime AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AND ''' + CAST(@LetterTDateTime AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''''
	END
	/**/
	IF ISNULL(@LetterPreNumber, '') <> ''	
	BEGIN
		SET @Term = @Term +
		' AND (LetterPreNumber = ''' + @LetterPreNumber + 
				''' Or LetterPreNumberII = ''' + @LetterPreNumber + ''') '
	END
	/**/
	IF ISNULL(@LetterNumber, '') <> ''	
	BEGIN
		SET @Term = @Term +
		' AND LetterNumber IN ( ' + @LetterNumber +')'
	END
	/**/
	IF ISNULL(@LetterNumberII, '') > ''	
	BEGIN
		SET @Term = @Term +
		' AND LetterNumberII IN ( ' + @LetterNumberII +')'
	END
	/**/
	IF ISNULL(@LetterAnnex, '') <> ''	
	BEGIN
		/**/
		IF @LetterAnnex = '-1'
		  SET @Term = @Term +
		  ' AND ISNULL(LetterAnnex, '''') = '''' '
		ELSE 
		IF @LetterAnnex = '-2'
		  SET @Term = @Term +
		  ' AND ISNULL(LetterAnnex, '''') <> '''' '	  
		/**/
		ELSE 
		IF @LetterAnnexLikely = '1'
			IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = (SELECT TOP 1 DefaultLanguageID FROM dbo.CrmsysSetting))
			BEGIN
				 SET @Term = @Term +
				' AND REPLACE(CAST(LetterAnnex as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610))  
				 Like (''%''+ N''' + @LetterAnnex + ''' + ''%'')'
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				 SET @Term = @Term +
				 ' AND (SELECT REPLACE(CAST(LetterAnnex as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610))'+ 
				 ' FROM dbo.SecLetters_Lang LLN WHERE LLN.LetterId = L.LetterId AND LLN.LanguageID = '+CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10))+')'+
				 ' Like (''%''+ N''' + @LetterAnnex + ''' + ''%'')'
			END
		ELSE 
			IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = (SELECT TOP 1 DefaultLanguageID FROM dbo.CrmsysSetting))
			BEGIN
				  SET @Term = @Term +
				' AND REPLACE(CAST(LetterAnnex as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)) = ''' + @LetterAnnex + ''''
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				 SET @Term = @Term +
				 ' AND (SELECT REPLACE(CAST(LetterAnnex as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610))'+ 
				 ' FROM dbo.SecLetters_Lang LLN WHERE LLN.LetterId = L.LetterId AND LLN.LanguageID = '
				 +CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10))+')= ''' + @LetterAnnex + ''''
			END
	
		
	END
	/**/
	IF ISNULL(@LetterCompanyNameId, '') <> '' AND ISNULL(@LetterCompanyNameId, '0') <> '0'
	BEGIN
		SET @Term = @Term +
		' AND LetterCompanyId IN ( ' + @LetterCompanyNameId +')'
	END
	/**/
	IF ISNULL(@LetterSubject, '') <> ''	
	BEGIN
		/**/
		IF @LetterSubject = '-1'
		  SET @Term = @Term +
		  ' AND ISNULL(LetterSubject, '''') = '''' '

		(SELECT [LettercmpNickName] FROM dbo.SecLetters_Lang LLN WHERE LLN.LetterId = L.LetterId AND LLN.LanguageID = '+CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10))+') [LettercmpNickName],
		[LettercmpPersonId],
		(SELECT [LettercmpPersonNickName] FROM dbo.SecLetters_Lang LLN WHERE LLN.LetterId = L.LetterId AND LLN.LanguageID = '+CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10))+') [LettercmpPersonNickName],
		[LettercmpProjectId],
		(SELECT [LetterSubject] FROM dbo.SecLetters_Lang LLN WHERE LLN.LetterId = L.LetterId AND LLN.LanguageID = '+CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10))+') [LetterSubject],
		(SELECT [LetterHeader] FROM dbo.SecLetters_Lang LLN WHERE LLN.LetterId = L.LetterId AND LLN.LanguageID = '+CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10))+') [LetterHeader],
		(SELECT [LetterContext] FROM dbo.SecLetters_Lang LLN WHERE LLN.LetterId = L.LetterId AND LLN.LanguageID = '+CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10))+') [LetterContext],
		(SELECT [LetterFooter] FROM dbo.SecLetters_Lang LLN WHERE LLN.LetterId = L.LetterId AND LLN.LanguageID = '+CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10))+') [LetterFooter],
		(SELECT [LetterSigningI] FROM dbo.SecLetters_Lang LLN WHERE LLN.LetterId = L.LetterId AND LLN.LanguageID = '+CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10))+') [LetterSigningI],
		(SELECT [LetterSigningII] FROM dbo.SecLetters_Lang LLN WHERE LLN.LetterId = L.LetterId AND LLN.LanguageID = '+CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10))+') [LetterSigningII],
		(SELECT [LetterSigningIII] FROM dbo.SecLetters_Lang LLN WHERE LLN.LetterId = L.LetterId AND LLN.LanguageID = '+CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10))+') [LetterSigningIII],
		(SELECT [LetterSigningIV] FROM dbo.SecLetters_Lang LLN WHERE LLN.LetterId = L.LetterId AND LLN.LanguageID = '+CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10))+') [LetterSigningIV],
		[LetterPrintFormatId],
		[LetterLastPrintDate],
		[LetterLastPrintTime],
		[LetterLastPrintDateTime],
		[LetterIsVerified],
		[LetterVerifierId],
		[LetterVerifierRequestId],
		[LetterVerifiedRegisterDate],
		[LetterVerifiedRegisterDatePersian],
		[FirstRegisterDate],
		[FirstRegisterDatePersian],
		[FirstRegisterUserId],
		[UserId],
		[Year1],
		[Month1],
		[RegisterDate],
		[RegisterDatePersian],
		[GUId],
		[AppType],
		[LanguageID]
		,[LetterNumberCounterId]
		,(SELECT [LtrNumCntTitle] FROM [dbo].[SecLetterNumberCounter_Lang] LNC WHERE LNC.[LetterNumberCounterId] = L.[LetterNumberCounterId] AND LNC.LanguageID = '+CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10))+') [LetterNumberCounter]
	 '
	
	END

	/**/
	IF(@PageNumber > 0)
	BEGIN
		SET @FinalQry = 
				';WITH CtePaging  AS (' + 
				'SELECT 						
				*,
				Row_number() OVER('+ @OrderBy +') AS RowNum,
				CASE  WHEN (COUNT(*) OVER() - ((COUNT(*) OVER() / ' + CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ') * ' + CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ') ) = 0
					THEN
						COUNT(*) OVER() / ' + CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '
					ELSE
						(COUNT(*) OVER() / ' + CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ') + 1
					END
				AS AllPage
				FROM(' + 
					@Qry + @From + @Term
				+') AS temTbl' 													
				+') 
				SELECT 
					* 	
				FROM   CtePaging 
				WHERE  RowNum BETWEEN ( ' + CAST(@PageNumber AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '- 1 ) * ' + CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' + 1 AND ' + CAST(@PageNumber AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' * ' + CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' ORDER BY RowNum'
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @FinalQry = @Qry + '
								,Row_number() OVER(' + @OrderBy + ') AS RowNum
								,1 AS AllPage' + @From + @Term + ' ORDER BY RowNum'
	END
	/**/

	EXECUTE(@FinalQry)		
END

							WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 23)

							) 
						) WHERE 1=1'

				Row_number() OVER(ORDER BY CompanyId DESC) AS RowNum,
				CASE  WHEN (COUNT(*) OVER() - ((COUNT(*) OVER() / ' + CAST(@PageSize AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ') * ' + CAST(@PageSize AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ') ) = 0
					THEN
						COUNT(*) OVER() / ' + CAST(@PageSize AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '
					ELSE
						(COUNT(*) OVER() / ' + CAST(@PageSize AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ') + 1
					END
				AS AllPage,							
				*				
				FROM(' + 
					@clmn + @FROM + @Term + @cmpDataPermision /*+ ' ORDER BY CompanyId DESC'*/	
				+') AS temTbl' 													
				+') 
				SELECT 
					* 	
				FROM   CtePaging 
				WHERE  RowNum BETWEEN ( ' + CAST(@PageNumber AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '- 1 ) * ' + CAST(@PageSize AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ' + 1 AND ' + CAST(@PageNumber AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + ' * ' + CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) 

EXECUTE (@Mainqry)
/*=============================== Paging END =================================*/

				' OR  ISNULL((SELECT REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(REPLACE(prsFName, '' '' ,'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang PPLN WHERE PPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID AND PPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''') Like ''%' + 
				REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))  +'%'''+	  
				' OR CP.prsTel Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR CP.prsTelII Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR CP.prsMobile Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR CP.prsFax Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.CmpCode Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.cmpNationalCode Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR  REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(REPLACE(C.cmpNameEng, '' '' ,'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' + 
				REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))  +'%'''+	     
				' OR CP.prsNationalCode Like ''%' + @Val  +'%''' +
				')' /*AI14020621 - Add parentheses*/
	   /*AI14010529*/
END		

/*AI14010529*/
SET @from = ' From dbo.CrmCompany C 
			LEFT JOIN 
				dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP 
					ON (C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId  
						AND	(CP.[FirstRegisterUserId] IN 
							(SELECT ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' UNION 
							SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers 
							WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 23)
							OR (CP.[FirstRegisterUserId] IS NULL)
							) 
						)'
/*SET @from = ' FROM dbo.CRMCompany C LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId' AI14010529*/

/*--*/
/*--*/
/*--*/
IF @cmpDataPermision <> ''
BEGIN
EXECUTE(
'SELECT * FROM (' +  /*AI14020621 - Add this line*/
' SELECT * FROM ( ' +
@fld+ @from+ @term +@cmpDataPermision +
' ) C union ' + 
' SELECT * FROM ( ' +
@fldLimit+ @from+ @term +
') d 
) tmp /*AI14020621 - Add this line*/
where CompanyID IN ( SELECT CompanyId '+ /*User CompanyID instead of CmpId*/ /*AI14020621 - "In" instead of "NOT IN"*/
@from+ @term +@cmpDataPermision +' )'
)
END
ELSE
EXECUTE(@fld+ @from+ @term)

END








GO
